## 11.20 Three pictures from C2W4: **the fair fight**, **the empty room**, and **the cell you can weigh twice** **[plain-language records — new C2W4, filed 2026-08-01]**

**Why this section exists.** C2W4 did two things this primer has to record for anyone arriving new. It **gave five competing memories every advantage the program could afford** — the full tuning grid, five times the budget, a fair selection rule — and asked whether any of them, or ours, beats a plain table holding the same bytes. And it **put our full memory inside a real language model on real bytes for the first time**. ⭐ **The physics worth keeping is not either scoreboard; it is the third record, which explains why "matched" is a word with two meanings and why one of them is unreachable for most architectures.** ⛔⛔ **Read this first: every number from the in-block experiment is at TOY scale (0.16 M parameters, 409 600 tokens, 3 seeds), the 26–47 M run was NOT RUN, and *"needs > 500 M"* is unavailable by pre-commitment.** Registry **N191–N210**; ledger ⟲ C2W4.

### Record 17 — **The fair fight** (what happens when you spend a wave trying to beat yourself)

> **The fair fight.** Here is the audit, and it is the whole of the tier-i paper. Take five published memory architectures — two test-time-training cells, three delta-rule cells — and give each one **exactly the same number of bytes of state** that our store carries. Now write down, for each, the **plain table** you could have stored in those same bytes, and read it with the cheapest possible reader: **zero extra bytes, no learning, nearest neighbour and friends.** ⭐ **Nobody beats the table. Not one of the five, and not us.** ⛔ **And the obvious objection is the right one: *you tuned yourself and not them.* So we tuned them — the full grid, five times the budget, and a selection rule that picks on held-out data rather than on the thing being optimised. The answer did not move: 0 of 5, margins −0.26 to −0.46 at nine seeds, every one more than four standard errors below zero.** ⭐⭐ **Two facts make that a measurement rather than a boast: the widened grid's new points are chosen in ZERO of forty-five cells, and cutting one arm's training loss by 64 % moved its score by less than one standard error. The competition is not learning-rate-limited. It is generalisation-limited, and so are we.**

**The same thing in symbols and numbers** (`bprime-rivals` §1.1; `bprime-rivals-f3` §1/§3; `aggregate@base`, iso-state **1364 float32 = 5456 B**, head widths **29/12/36** fixed in the prereg *before* any run). Write $\mathcal T_{\rm raw}$ for the raw-metric table at matched bytes and $R_{+0}$ for the best zero-extra-byte reader of it:

$$\text{margin}(\text{arm}) \;=\; \text{score}(\text{arm}) \;-\; \text{score}\big(R_{+0}[\mathcal T_{\rm raw}]\big) \;<\; 0 \quad \text{for all five arms and for the CLU.}$$

| arm | +0 B margin (n=3) | **raw-table margin, pooled n = 9** | rescued at n = 9? |
|---|---|---|---|
| TTT-Linear | −0.0523 | **−0.4602 ± 0.1038** | ⚠ **UNPINNED** |
| TTT-MLP | −0.2284 | **−0.4425 ± 0.0869** | ⛔ **never, in any configuration** |
| DeltaNet | −0.0047 | **−0.2732 ± 0.0395** | ✅ (⛔ **its C2W4 never-quote is RETRACTED**) |
| Gated DeltaNet | **+0.0448** | **−0.2600 ± 0.0278** | ✅ (⚠ its +0 B margin crosses ≤ 0 at n=9) |
| Gated DeltaNet-2 | **+0.0445** | **−0.2592 ± 0.0292** | ✅ (+0.047 ± 0.028 = **a tie, not a win**) |
| **CLU** | **−0.3180 ± 0.0804** | (= its own) | ✅ |

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐ **Two things in this record are contributions rather than results, and they are the parts a physicist should take away.** **(1) A control can be wrong in your favour and you will not notice.** Our own pre-registered launder read each rival's byte-matched table **through that rival's own projections** — and that costs the table **0.26–0.94**, because those projections were trained for a recurrence and a single stored value is out of distribution for a head trained on averaged reads. **Run only that control and you publish dividends of +0.9 to +1.0 that do not exist.** ⛔ ***That is laundering by omission, and we found it on the competition's side of our own audit.*** **(2) A verdict has a power, and ours did not have enough.** The gate that decides whether an arm is even alive (*is it better than its own blank store?*) has a **control whose seed-to-seed spread exceeds the effect it gates** — so at three seeds it is a coin flip, and at nine it stabilises and **retracts one of our own published prohibitions.** ⭐ ***Quote a verdict only at the power where it is stable*** — and if the control is noisier than the signal, fix the control, not the threshold.

**Status: [measured, 5 arms × 9 seeds, one uniform protocol; the headline survives a full lr×wd grid, 5× budget and held-out selection] · [⛔ ONE synthetic family (`aggregate`), $d_{\rm in} = 5$, ~10-token streams — NOTHING transfers to a language-model claim] · [⛔ no Titans arm (no code, no numeric chunk size, no seeds) ⇒ P3 is NOT-RUN, not refuted; SDM needs unavailable hardware; Mamba-2/GRU/SWA adjudicated by equations only, never blurred with the measured three] · [⛔ every rival number quotes POOLED n = 9].** **Reading pointers:** Record 13 (the byte floor that makes "matched bytes" definable at all), Record 14 (the ruler that measures the task — why only one family survives to be measured on), registry **N191 / N192 / N193 / N194 / N195**, charter §A14.2/§A14.3.

### Record 18 — **The empty room** (a memory the model could not tell was there)

> **The empty room.** For the first time, the full memory — every lever on, nothing stubbed — was placed inside a small language model reading real bytes, one read and one write per 32-token chunk, with the strict rule that a chunk never sees its own summary. ⭐ **The good news is structural and it is a first: the gradients flow all the way through. Text → embedding → the store's physics → the trajectory read → loss, end to end, and the friction and inertia knobs receive gradient exactly where thirty waves of theory said they would — through the trajectory, and NOWHERE ELSE (their settled-point gradients are zero to the last bit).** ⛔⛔ **The bad news is the first-order finding. Delete everything the memory ever stored, and the model's loss does not change — not approximately: `0.00e+00`, `−5.96e-08`, `0.00e+00` across three seeds.** Ask the store to recall its own items at their own addresses and it scores **exactly chance**, and so does a store that was never written. ⭐ **And the reflex explanation is refuted by measurement: sixteen times the write budget buys 1.33× the well depth and ZERO recall, while the read at full budget converges perfectly.** *The wells were never dug.* **The suspect — named, untested, and deliberately not chased mid-experiment — is where the atoms START: 128 of them scattered across a ball, which a handful of write steps cannot gather into a well at the target.**

**The same thing in numbers** (`cluformer-pilot` §5/§5.1/§5.2, 3 seeds, TOY scale):

| what was varied | 4 inner write steps | 16 | **64** |
|---|---|---|---|
| self-probe acquisition | 0.250 | 0.250 | **0.250** (chance = 0.250) |
| median fitted well depth $D$ | 0.0331 | 0.0448 | **0.0442** — *saturated* |

against the shipped store's fitted $D = 0.46$–$0.80$ — **10–18× too shallow, and more steps do not fix it.** Meanwhile the read is fine: $\rho_{\rm conv} = 0.834$ at the affordable 48 Verlet steps but **$2.6\times10^{-7}$ at the shipped 1200**, reproducing the standalone harness exactly. ⇒ ⛔ **neither the read nor the write is starved of STEPS.**

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐ **The transferable sentence is about experimental design, and it cost 1.35 CPU-hours to learn instead of ~108 A100-hours: when a system produces no effect, find out whether the effect is ABSENT or merely SMALL before you scale.** Three independent measurements agreed here — a deletion control, a self-probe, and a budget sweep — and all three said *absent*. ⭐ **The corollary is the flat curve: the anytime read spends 16× the compute and moves the loss by $7.5\times10^{-4}$, because *a memory that carries nothing cannot be read better by reading it longer.*** ⛔ **And the honest scoreboard travels with it: at this scale the memory is a NET COST against simply deleting it (+0.0070 ± 0.0055 bpc, 3/3 seeds) and it loses to a size-matched GRU (+0.0106 ± 0.0057, 3/3); dynamic evaluation helps every arm identically, so it had no advantage to erase.** ⭐ *The engineer pre-registered that direction of failure and reported it happening — which is the only reason this record is evidence rather than an anecdote.*

**Status: [⛔ TOY scale (0.16 M params, 2 layers, 200 steps, 409 600 tokens), 3 seeds — an INSTRUMENT READING, never a 26–47 M number] · [⛔ S4, the cluster run, NOT RUN: no route from the agent; job script ready and Head-submittable; DF1/DF2/DF3 NOT SCORED at pilot scale; *"needs >500 M"* NOT AVAILABLE] · [⚠ monitor #13 DEMOTES every reading here to formally NON-PROMOTABLE under N94 — stated, not hidden] · [⚠ 5 of 14 monitors trip and none trips silently; 4 are INAPPLICABLE, which is a third state and never a pass] · [⚠ the placement hypothesis is UNTESTED and its three candidate fixes are named and UNBUILT].** **Nomenclature (Def-2):** the trainable $M$ here is **inertial mass**; **spectral $\mu$ does not enter this record.** **Reading pointers:** §11.4 (the gradient dichotomy — why the trajectory channel is the only one), Record 10 (the one-way mirror, and its $e^{-C}$ update), Record 19 (the matching that made the comparison legal), registry **N196 / N197 / N199 / N200 / N201**, charter §A13 (tier iii) / §A14.3.

> ⛔ **"Atom placement at init" is refuted as the cause.** Setting the localized init (`atom_local_radius`) at its designed band is **worse than doing nothing** in a streaming block — **up to −6 orders of well depth** — because **the write site is chosen by the controller at write time and is unknown at init.** ⛔ **The trajectory write term is bit-identically inert** at 3.9× the wall clock (its gradient is ~1e-13 at a flat init, and the shipped sign-SGD write takes the *sign* of it).
> ⭐ **What does wake the wells is placement-at-WRITE plus a raised hinge margin: depth 0.0288 → 0.1668** — and ⛔ **the 0.045 saturation this record reports was never the budget: it is the shipped `write_margin = 0.15`** (×10 steps with placement buys 1.03×). ⛔⛔ **But depth is not free: at margin 1.0 the between-item `q*` discrimination collapses 0.114 → 0.054 — *a deeper well at a shared payload location is a WORSE memory.***
> ⭐⭐ **And the real cause is downstream of the physics — see Record 22.** The dynamics **do** deliver the payload (30–50 % of the way into `q*`; a blank store delivers exactly 0.000); **ψ then compresses it 7–25×**, to a between-item spread the same size as the live-vs-blank difference. ⇒ ⭐ **that is why acquisition sat at *exactly* chance rather than near it.** ⛔ **One further warning this record could not have seen: 200 steps of OUTER training drive the wells to 4.95e-63** (Record 21's coda; **N223**). **Registry N221 / N222 / N223; charter §A20.3(a).**

### Record 19 — **The cell you can weigh twice** (why "matched" is two different words)

> **The cell you can weigh twice.** To claim a memory helps, you must swap it for a fair rival and show the loss gets worse. But *fair* has two meanings that usually disagree. You can match the **parameters** — how much is learned — or you can match the **state** — how much is carried from one moment to the next. ⭐ **For most recurrent cells those two numbers are wildly different, and you cannot hit both.** A GRU's parameters grow like the *square* of its state, so matching our store's parameters leaves it carrying **a hundred times less state**, and matching its state would require a cell of **79 million parameters — three times an entire model.** ⛔ **So a single "matched GRU" column is not a control; it is a choice of which advantage to give away.** ⭐⭐ **Our store has an unusual property that makes it honest: its parameters ARE its state. And exactly one published family shares that property — the test-time-training cells, whose slow weights are their memory — so a TTT-class cell can be matched on BOTH axes at once, to under one part in a thousand. That, and not the GRU, is the two-sided control.**

**The same thing in numbers** (`cluformer-pilot` §2/§2.1, learned-initial-state rule: *an initialisation is PARAMETERS; only the per-sequence deviation is STATE*):

| cell | parameters | state bytes | $\text{params}/\text{state}$ |
|---|---|---|---|
| **CLU store** | 8 616 | **20 576** | **1.675** |
| GRU, matched **params** | 8 682 (**+0.77 %**) | 204 (⛔ **CLU = 100.9×**) | 170.2 |
| GRU, matched **state** | ⛔ **79 469 668 — NOT CONSTRUCTED** | 20 576 | — |
| **TTT-class, matched BOTH** | 8 620 (**+0.046 %**) | 20 556 (**−0.097 %**) | 1.677 |

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐ **State which axis your baseline is matched on, or your baseline is not a control.** The accepted practice from this wave is to **bracket**: publish *both* one-sided GRU columns **and** the two-sided TTT arm, never a single one-sided match. ⭐ **And the ratio itself is the physics: a cell whose parameters are its state can be weighed twice and give the same answer; a cell whose parameters are quadratic in its state cannot.** ⚠ **It also gets easier with scale in our favour — at pilot geometry the ratio approaches 1.02, so the TTT match tightens and the GRU's impossibility hardens (100.9× → 566×).** ⛔ **What the same ledger says about us is not flattering, and it belongs in the same breath: at our own state bytes a plain table would hold 1 714 rows where the store holds 8 items.**

**Status: [⭐ resolved BY CONSTRUCTION — arithmetic, not measurement; both columns published in the swap ledger] · [⚠ $\text{params}/\text{state} = 1.675$ is a TOY figure (the read-out net is a larger fraction of the cell there); 1.02 is the pilot-geometry prediction] · [⭐ the shared shell is bit-identical on every arm and asserted at runtime — only the memory cell differs] · [⚠ inherits Record 18's scope: no tier-iii performance claim exists].** **Reading pointers:** Record 13 and its C2W4 update (the byte ledger this is an instance of), Record 17 (the same matching discipline applied to five published rivals), registry **N198 / N191**, charter §A14.2.

## 11.21 Three pictures from C2W5: **four particles in two rooms**, **the settle that merges**, and **the funnel** **[plain-language records — new C2W5, filed 2026-08-01]**

**Why this section exists — and the one sentence to read before the three records.** C2W5 asked the composition question for the first time with a real store: *write items into shared wells, then query a combination nobody wrote, and see whether a physics-organized store answers better than a non-physics one.* ⛔⛔ **It never got to that comparison.** The store's read could not express the answer, no organizer of any class could, and the wave's real product is knowing **which component** failed. ⛔⛔ **THE BINDING FORM OF THAT RESULT (charter §A20.2, Head + Advisor, and it governs every sentence in this section): the `P`-particle occupancy read protocol is refuted at `P = 4`. The compositional FAMILY is not refuted — it is 27.2 % decodable from the very inputs every arm saw. And the PHYSICS is not adjudicated: the organizer swap was never run.** Registry **N212–N224**; ledger ⟲ C2W5.

### Record 20 — **Four particles in two rooms** (the read that could not say the answer, and the check that costs seconds)

> **Four particles in two rooms.** The task is deliberately simple. There are 32 rooms; every item lives in one of them and carries a value. A *query* names **four** rooms, and the correct answer is **the sum of the four values** — for a combination of rooms that was never written together. To answer, the memory launches **four probe particles** and lets them roll downhill; wherever each lands, it picks up that room's value, and the four are added. ⭐ **So the whole scheme has a precondition nobody had ever measured: the four particles must land in four *different* rooms.** ⛔⛔ **They land in 2.20.** All four land in four distinct rooms on **5 % of queries**, and the number of queries where the set of occupied rooms was *exactly* the right set is **zero out of 2 560.** ⇒ ***You cannot add four numbers you never collected.*** ⭐⭐ **And here is what turns a dead end into a design brief: the information was there the whole time.** A decoder allowed to look at the four launch positions and compare them against all 35 960 possible room-sets gets the answer **exactly right on 27.2 % of unseen queries** — and **100 %** if you hand it the noiseless code. ⇒ ⛔ ***The family is solvable. The read is what fails.*** ⚠ **That 27.2 % is a REFERENCE CEILING from a decoder outside the allowed class — it is what the launch information supports, never a bar any arm is scored against.**

**The same thing in numbers** (`orgdiv-null-arms` §1/§1.1/§3/§4; `N_a = 32` wells, `F = 4`, `K = 128` written items, `d = 4`, `m = 8`, `P = 4` frozen launch offsets, 5 seeds, 2 560 unseen queries; chance $= 3.906\times10^{-4}$, bar $=$ chance $+\,0.05 = 0.05039$):

| quantity | measured |
|---|---|
| distinct wells occupied by the $P=4$ particles (raw launch) | **2.202 ± 0.02** of the **4** required |
| $P(\ge F$ distinct wells$)$ — ⭐ **this is `K0`** | **0.050** |
| exact-set occupancy (occupied set $==A(x)$) | ⛔ **0.0000 / 2 560** |
| **`null*`** = grid-max over **584 registered configs × 5 seeds**, all 5 organizer arms | ⛔ **0.00117** (**43× below the bar**) |
| out-of-class combinatorial decoder, **as launched** / **noiseless** | **0.2719 ± 0.0126** / **1.0000 ± 0.0000** |
| internal-validity anchors: N1 fits its own train split · N4 $k{=}1$ on SEEN · shuffle-$\varphi$ launder · capacity flatness | **1.0000** · **1.0000** · **≤ 0.00039** · **0.0000** |

⭐ **The `K0` line is the transferable one, and it is one probability:**

$$K0:\quad \Pr\big[\,|\{\,\text{well}(q_0 + \delta_p)\,\}_{p=1}^{P}|\;\ge\;F\,\big]\;\;=\;\;0.050 ,$$

**computable from the launch offsets $\{\delta_p\}$ and the well positions alone — no store, no training, seconds of arithmetic.**

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐ **Two lessons, and the second is the one to steal.** **(1) Say which component failed, or you have not measured anything.** *"No organizer clears"* and *"the read cannot express the answer"* are the same table of numbers and completely different conclusions — the first closes a research direction, the second hands you two concrete requirements: **address `F` distinct wells**, and **do not quantise away the continuous launch coordinate** (⚠ *quantisation is the named destructive step: the gradient-placed, VQ and static-geometric arms all replace the launch point with a codebook centre, discarding exactly the coordinate the 27.2 % decoder uses*). **(2) Build the kill-condition's PRE-condition too.** The program's standing doctrine is *build the kill-condition before the thing it can kill*; `K0` is the cheaper sibling — ⭐ ***a kill-condition tells you when to stop; a pre-condition tells you not to start.*** ⛔ **And the sharpest single control in the wave belongs here: an organizer with the identical parameterisation and FIVE TIMES the bytes fits 1.0000 of its training items and scores 0.0000 on held-out combinations. It memorises perfectly and composes not at all** — so *"nothing worked"* is a statement about the problem, not about the optimiser.

**Status: [measured, 5 organizer arms × 584 configs × 5 seeds, one frozen $\varphi$ (576 B, byte-compared) and bit-identical launch points] · [⭐ the pre-registered §7.3 branch — *ceiling high + arms at zero ⇒ PROTOCOL refutation, not family refutation* — was committed BEFORE the harness ran; prereg score 13 ✅ / 1 ◐ / 1 ⛔] · [⛔ `null*`'s grid-max is an ORACLE-SELECTED UPPER BOUND, never any arm's score] · [⛔ the physics arm is NOT in this comparison — it died at its own K5 (Record 21), so `OD`/`OD_min` do not exist and the organizer swap is DEFERRED, not cancelled] · [⛔ $P>4$ is an out-of-protocol diagnostic: more particles fix addressability (2.19 → 4.34 wells) and information (ceiling 0.288 → 0.938) but NOT precision (0.40 at every $P$) ⇒ $P$ alone is not the fix] · [⛔ the mandatory re-labelling, §A20.2, applies to every quotation of this record].** **Nomenclature (Def-2):** no mass statement is made here; **inertial $M = I$** in the read budget, **spectral $\mu$ does not enter.** **Reading pointers:** Record 21 (why the settle makes this worse), Record 15 (a memory a table can copy — the settled-point precursor), registry **N212 / N214 / N215 / N216**, charter **§A20.1–§A20.3**.

> **1. ⭐⭐ The precondition is met and the expressivity bar CLEARS for the first time.** The replacement read (Record 25) commits to a cardinality: **`F_hat` = 4.542 ± 0.122**, asserted-set size **4.194 ± 0.224** (iteration 1: 5.79 ± 0.90, never 4), **exactly the right cardinality on 39.4 % of queries**, and **exact-set occupancy 0.0023 → 0.065625 ± 0.023876 (mean − 2 SE = 0.0417 > the 0.02 bar) — 28×.** ⇒ ***"you cannot add four numbers you never collected" has been answered: now it collects them.***
> **2. ⛔⛔ And the store still subtracts value from its own launches.** Against the **same head's own launches with the landscape deleted**, the full read scores **−0.007031 ± 0.001562, negative on 5 of 5 seeds** — and the **designed, untrained head beats the trained one** (0.0695 vs 0.0539). ⛔ **`R1` / the `F`-commitment / *"the cardinality mechanism works"* may NEVER be quoted without that clause in the same paragraph.**
> **3. ⚠ `K0` — this record's own transferable idea — needs a caveat it did not have.** It is a **REACHABILITY** statement and nothing more: a collapsed head scores **`K0 = 1.000`** while **every reader reads 0.0000** (measured), and the C2W7 claim cell's own PASS is a **mean** (0.9070 ± 0.0125; per-seed **3 of 5** clear the 0.90 bar). ⛔ **Never quote `K0` alone as "the read is healthy".**
> **4. ⛔ `null*` = 0.00117 was put in question and it did NOT move.** A zero-parameter-reader re-score of this record's own cells returned **SURVIVES**: `null*` unmoved, N1's 1.0000/0.0000 unmoved, largest identity-minus-fitted gain anywhere **+0.0023** (Record 26). ⭐ **The new precondition that rides with it: `K6` — the fraction of queries whose asserted set is already exactly right — reported BEFORE any reader is fitted.**

### Record 21 — **The settle that merges** (relaxation is a contraction, and a set-valued answer loses members to it)

> **The settle that merges.** A physicist's instinct about the read in Record 20 is that letting the particles settle should *help*: rolling downhill is exactly how a noisy launch point becomes a clean answer. ⭐ **For a single-answer query that instinct is right. For a set-valued query it is exactly backwards, and now we have the number.** Before the settle, the four particles sit in **2.20** distinct wells; after it, **1.70**. Score how often a particle is in one of the four *correct* rooms and the same thing shows up: **0.406 from the raw launch geometry, 0.297 after the physics settle.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **The relaxation destroys 27 % of the set information the launch already carried.** **The mechanism is one line: a damped roll is a contraction onto minima, so two particles launched into the same catchment arrive at *one* answer — the settle MERGES particles into shared basins, and every merge costs the query a member.** ⭐ **And there is a scoring lesson hiding in the same measurement, which the program has now made a standing rule: a blank store already beats the naive baseline** — because the wells sit at the query codes, the launch geometry alone is a matched filter. ⇒ ⛔ ***Occupancy is scored against the blank store, never against `F/N_a`.***

**The same thing in numbers** (`orgdiv-cat-test` §1.2 + reconciliation 4; `orgdiv-null-arms` §3, which rebuilt the statistic independently on its own rig):

| statistic, unseen queries | raw launch | **after the settle** |
|---|---|---|
| distinct wells occupied ($F=4$ required) | **2.202 ± 0.02** | ⛔ **1.70** |
| occupancy precision (particle in a well of $A(x)$) | **0.4061** (null-arms: **0.4106**) | ⛔ **0.297** |
| ⛔ naive baseline that must NOT be used | $F/N_a = 0.125$ | — it is beaten by a **blank** store |

**Read budget for both rows** (state it, always — every $\gamma$ statement is budget-scoped): two-phase damped Verlet, $(\gamma_{\rm address}, N) = (0.05, 400) \to (\gamma_{\rm read}, N) = (0.02, 800)$, $dt = 0.05$, `kinetic_mode = newtonian_learned`, $M = I$.

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐ **The design consequence is sharp and it is what §A20.3 acts on: any read that must express `F` distinct answers is fighting its own relaxation, so the fix is a different read, not a tuned one.** The replacement being scoped emits **k full particles from a learned launch head** (k positions, inertial masses, frictions), uses **overlap as confidence** (confident ⇒ the k collapse to `F` unique answers; unfamiliar ⇒ k scattered guesses), and makes **dedupe/evolve-unique a controller verb that doubles as the compute dial** — with a **soft** occupancy signal, because ⛔ **hard assignments do not backpropagate and, measured, the organizer is untrainable until wells exist (gradient norms `1e-10 → O(1)` across the write).** ⚠ **And the coda that decides whether any of it survives training: 200 steps of OUTER training drive the store's well depth from 0.029 to 4.95e-63** — placement + margin survives the same training at 0.0616 ± 0.037. ⭐ **That is now instrumented in flight (`store_health_probe`, on by default) with a pre-registered abort: below 1e-3 absolute, or below 0.1× its own untrained depth on two consecutive reads, the run PAUSES and reports a mechanism instead of burning an allocation.**

**Status: [measured, 5 seeds, 2-SE-separated, and independently reproduced on a second rig (0.4061 vs 0.4106)] · [⭐ standing doctrine adopted: occupancy/co-activation precision is scored against the BLANK STORE / raw launch geometry, never `F/N_a`] · [⚠ it is a statement about a SET-VALUED occupancy read, not about settled-point reads in general — for those, O1/N206 already applies] · [⛔ the well-destruction coda is TOY scale (0.16 M, 3 seeds) and its CAUSE is unowned: the one gradient probe separating φ-drift from amplitude suppression is unrun] · [⛔ the physics arm these numbers come from ran COLLAPSED (`S_eff` 34.1/51.2/36.6 vs band [8,16]) and UNTUNED — the only quotable verdict form is *"physics, untuned and collapsed, does not clear its own K5"*].** **Nomenclature (Def-2):** $\gamma$ here is **friction**, not a mass; the read uses **inertial $M = I$**; **spectral $\mu$ does not enter.** **Reading pointers:** §1.8 (friction with an exact accounting), §11.4 (Prop 5 — dissipation is what kills address gradients: the same contraction, seen on the gradient side), Record 20, registry **N212 / N213 / N223**, charter **§A20.3(b)–(d)**.

> **1. ⛔ At a `k`-particle multiplicity head the settle SCATTERS instead of merging.** The head allocates its 12 particles onto **4.834 ± 0.125** distinct wells; the settle delivers them to **5.668 ± 0.396**. *Particles committed to the same well end in different basins.* Raw occupancy precision falls **0.4578 ± 0.0260 → 0.4107 ± 0.0191**.
> **2. ⭐⭐ And the cause is now named: the store's attractors sit 1.09 — about 1.4 well-spacings — from its designed anchors**, so a launch aimed at anchor *j* can fall into a neighbouring basin. A counting code is exactly the representation this corrupts: `3/3/3/3` becomes `3/2/3/2/2`, so the per-well count stops landing on 1.
> **3. ⛔ The registered falsifier is blind by sign.** `R2` fires only if **settled < launched** — the merging direction above — so at the multiplicity head it **does not fire while the settle is the thing corrupting the answer.** ⭐ **Standing lesson: a falsifier inherited from a previous read protocol is not automatically a falsifier for the next one.**
> **4. ⭐ The design consequence, which is where the programme now goes:** the fix is **store-side — make the write objective place the attractors AT the designed anchors** — not another read. ⛔ **The read track is PAUSED; there is no third read iteration.**

### Record 22 — **The funnel** (the physics delivered the payload; the read-out threw it away)

> **The funnel.** When a memory produces no effect, there are two very different reasons, and until this wave we could not tell them apart: either **the physics never carried the information**, or **it carried it and the read-out discarded it.** The measurement that separates them is simple. Look at where the read's particle actually ends up — specifically at the *payload* coordinate of its settled position — and compare it with the value that was written. ⭐ **The dynamics deliver: the settled coordinate travels about 30 % of the way to the true payload at the shipped setting, ~48 % with better placement, and overshoots to 130–175 % at a high write margin — while a blank store delivers exactly 0.000.** ⛔⛔ **Then the read-out net compresses it away.** The decoded payloads for three different stored items span a range of **0.0085** where the true payloads span **0.2277** — a **7–25× compression** — and, worse, the spread *between different items* is the **same size as the difference between reading the live store and reading an empty one.** ⇒ ⭐⭐ **That is the quantitative form of "the read does not carry the payload", and it explains why in-block acquisition sat at *exactly* chance rather than near it: the nearest-stored-payload assignment becomes a fixed permutation of a nearly constant vector, which yields exactly one hit per lane — `acq = mean(1/n_live) = chance`, identically.**

**The same thing in numbers** (`pilot-placement-probe` §6.1/§6.2, lane 0, seed 0, 3 live items; TOY scale, 0.16 M params, 3 seeds):

| cell | $q^*[\text{payload}]$, live store | true payloads | fraction delivered | decoded range | **compression** |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `baseline` | −0.233 / −0.181 / −0.211 | −0.622 / −0.779 / −0.850 | **~30 %** | **0.0085** | **≈ 27×** |
| `h1b_r0.3` (placement) | −0.411 / −0.297 / −0.371 | (same) | **~48 %** | 0.0065 | ≈ 35× |
| `h1b_m1.0` (margin 1.0) | −1.089 / −1.090 / −1.143 | (same) | **130–175 % (overshoot)** | 0.0117 | ≈ 19× |
| blank store | **0.000** on all items | — | **0** | — | — |

with live-vs-blank decode $\max|\Delta| = $ **0.0031 / 0.0061 / 0.0063** — ⛔ **the same order as the between-item decode spread itself.**

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐ **The general lesson is a diagnostic order, and it is cheap: when a system produces no effect, decompose the pipeline and ask each stage whether it still has the signal — do not tune the whole thing.** Here the answer is unambiguous and it **exonerates the physics**: the store, the write and the dynamics all carry payload; **the read-out is the funnel.** ⭐ **That is why the fix is now the program's first experimental TODO** (`psi-payload-residual`), with an acceptance criterion sharp enough to fail: ***does the decoded spread reach the `q*` spread?*** — plus a **blocking leak check** (*a blank-store decode must stay at chance*, because a read-out that "improves" by encoding the query rather than the store has learned nothing). ⚠ **And it is priced into the scale run as a designed ablation rather than a hope: CSF3 run 1 uses the unfixed decoder and is the BASELINE LEG; run 2 is decoder-fixed with the same config otherwise.** ⛔ **No run-1 number is ever quoted as the scale verdict.**

**Status: [⛔ TOY scale (0.16 M params, 2 layers, 3 seeds), one lane, three live items — formally NON-PROMOTABLE under N94] · [⭐ the blank-store column is the control that makes it a delivery statement: 0.000, exactly] · [⛔ the fix is NOT BUILT — declared NOT-RUN, ~1-day build, acceptance §A20.3(a) verbatim, and it GATES CSF3 run 2] · [⭐ independently indicted from the other side: the cat test's read also fails to express what its geometry carries] · [⚠ ranked THIRD of three candidate mechanisms by the previous wave — the other two are refuted, N221].** **Nomenclature (Def-2):** $\psi$ is the permutation-invariant read-out (a `DeepSets` net), **not** a mass or a potential; the write's `margin` is a hinge, not a spectral gap; **spectral $\mu$ does not enter this record.** **Reading pointers:** Record 18 and its C2W5 update (the empty room this explains), §11.11 (the read-in $\varphi$ and its laundering control — the mirror-image question on the input side), registry **N221 / N222 / N223**, charter **§A20.3(a)** / **§A20.4**.

## 11.22 Two pictures from C2W6: **the memory nobody was reading** and **the door that was never welded shut** **[plain-language records — new C2W6, filed 2026-08-05]**

**Why this section exists — and the one sentence to read before the two records.** C2W6 was built to stop a destruction: the previous wave had measured a store's wells being annihilated by ordinary outer training, and this wave built the shield (a stop-gradient partition at the write boundary), built its kill-conditions as tests **before** any science cell, and then measured the thing it was protecting against. ⛔⛔ **The destruction does not reproduce at the configuration we ship.** ⭐ **What the wave bought instead is worth more than the shield: a mechanism that says *why* stores erode, and a correction to something the program had been documenting backwards for months.** ⛔ **Everything here is TOY (0.16 M params, 2 layers, `seq_len 512`, 3 paired seeds); every 4-inner-step reading is formally NON-PROMOTABLE under monitor #13/N94, and the 40-step (`w40`) pair is the undemoted cell.** Registry **N223 addendum / N225–N230**; charter **ADDENDUM 7 §A22–§A23**; ledger ⟲ C2W6.

> **⭐⭐ Update (C2W6 fold-2, 2026-08-06 — `c2w6-erosion-adjudication`, charter ADDENDUM 9 §A27.1; `doc-curator-c2w6-fold-2`). THE SENTENCE IN BOLD ABOVE IS WRONG, AND THE CORRECTION MAKES THE SECTION MORE INTERESTING, NOT LESS.** This section's framing claim — ⛔ *"The destruction does not reproduce at the configuration we ship"* — is **SUPERSEDED**, together with the wave's other approved wording *"N223's monotone collapse does not reproduce at the run-2 config."* **Both are NEVER-QUOTE.**
> ⭐⭐ **What actually happens: the destruction DOES reproduce — it is TRANSIENT rather than terminal.** An independent analyst re-derived every number in the wave from the raw artifacts (**21 records × 5 curve scalars, worst relative deviation `0.00e+00` — zero transcription or arithmetic error anywhere**) and then did the one thing nobody had done: **plotted the depth curve instead of its endpoints.** Over the first few hundred steps the wells **collapse and then partly refill** — depth falls to **0.9 %** of untrained at step **150** on one seed and **5.3 %** at step **275** on another (**112× and 19× losses**, against reading-to-reading noise of only **1.05–1.33×**; the third seed never dips), and then recovers by a geometric factor of **4.20**.
> ⭐⭐ **And the punchline is an instrument story: the banked measurement that started all of this was taken at 200 training steps — INSIDE the trough.** So the wave's registered decay test, *final depth ÷ depth at step 200*, had **its denominator sitting at the bottom of the dip on 2 of 3 seeds** — which is mechanically why it read **9.78×** and **0.53×** instead of the ≤0.3× that would have counted as decay. ⛔ **The test was measuring the bounce, not the fall.**
> ✅ **The one approved form from here on: *"N223's mechanism FIRES at the run-2 config but is NOT TERMINAL."*** ⭐ **This is an UPGRADE, not a retraction — it restores the mechanism as live.** ⛔⛔ **And nothing is re-scored: the E1 refutation stands at 0/3 (raw *and* after netting the designed decay), the gate verdict is unchanged, and the Head's re-scope premise — the BASELINE failed the test, not the shield — is independently confirmed.** ⚠ **Read Records 23 and 24 below with this correction applied; each carries its own dated update.**

### Record 23 — **The memory nobody was reading** (why stores erode, and what a shield can and cannot buy)

> **The memory nobody was reading.** A memory that sits inside a model being trained end-to-end is not a safe deposit box: the training signal reaches the memory's own parameters and can flatten the wells that hold its contents. The previous wave measured exactly that and it looked catastrophic. ⭐ **So this wave asked a sharper question: is that destruction an inevitable price of making the write differentiable — or is it what an optimiser does to a component that costs more than it returns?** The experiment is a single-knob ablation. Take the same model, the same seeds, the same 1000 training steps, and remove **the one channel by which the model can actually read the memory's payload**. ⛔⛔ **With the channel intact, the wells end at 71 % of their untrained depth. With it removed — the store still there, still written, simply useless to the loss — they end at 14 %, and the fraction of rewrites that damage a stored item rises from 23 % to 51 %.** ⇒ ⭐⭐ ***Erosion is a symptom of uselessness, not an intrinsic property of a differentiably-unrolled write.*** ⛔ **And the wave's own premise died in the same table: at the configuration we now ship, the catastrophic collapse simply does not happen.**

**The same thing in numbers** (`c2w6-anti-erosion` §3/§5.1, Advisor re-derivation §A22; the CSF3 **run-2** config — `atom_place_radius = 0.3`, `write_margin = 0.6`, ψ payload residual ON — 1000 outer steps, 3 paired seeds, depth = median fitted well depth on lane 0 of a fixed validation batch, sampled every 25 steps):

| arm | final ÷ untrained depth (**arith**) | (**geo**) | destructive-rewrite rate |
|---|---|---|---|
| partition OFF, residual **ON** (run-2) | **0.708 ± 0.57** | **0.327** | **0.228 ± 0.182** |
| partition **ON**, residual ON | 0.603 ± 0.23 | — | **0.009 ± 0.009** |
| ⛔DIAG partition OFF, residual **OFF** | ⛔ **0.141 ± 0.095** | **0.077** | ⛔ **0.512 ± 0.238** |

**The registered decay test, and the premise it retires** (E1: does the *unprotected* arm decay to ≤0.3× its own step-200 depth on ≥2 of 3 seeds? band ≤0.5×):

$$\text{E1: } \frac{D(1000)}{D(200)} \;=\; 9.782 \;/\; 0.9035 \;/\; 0.5305 \qquad \Rightarrow\qquad \textbf{REFUTED 0/3.}$$

⭐ **And the estimator lesson that rides on it, because depth is a positive quantity spanning decades** (within-arm seed spread **52–128×**). For per-seed depths $D_s$, the arithmetic mean $\frac{1}{n}\sum_s D_s$ is dominated by the largest seed, while the geometric mean $\big(\prod_s D_s\big)^{1/n} = \exp\!\big(\frac{1}{n}\sum_s \ln D_s\big)$ is the estimator the quantity's own scale calls for. ⛔ **The banked collapse figure "4.95e-63" is the ARITHMETIC mean of `1.439e-87 / 1.486e-62 / 2.493e-177` — three seeds spanning 114.8 orders of magnitude, geometric mean 3.764e-109** — *a single seed wearing a mean's clothes.* ⭐ **It is therefore a CENSORING STATEMENT and must be quoted as one:** ***"200 outer steps drive well depth below 1e-62 on 3/3 seeds (1.4e-87 / 1.5e-62 / 2.5e-177)."***

**And the shield itself, which ships — with its scope attached** (`erosion_partition`, a `stop_gradient` at the write boundary in three legs: the state a write returns, `init_state()`'s atom leaves, and `self._atoms` inside `write`):

| leg | measured |
|---|---|
| gradient to store parameters (`centers`, `log_width`, `amp`, **both layers**) | ⭐ **bitwise `0.0`** — not small, exactly zero |
| loss across arms | **bit-identical, `5.764082908630371`** (a backward-only intervention) |
| read channel (`log_mass`, both frictions, ψ, ψ residual gate) | ✅ **alive, unchanged** |
| bpc cost (ON − OFF), **w4** / **w40** | **−0.004853 ± 0.000780 (6.23 SE, 3/3 better)** / **+0.005676 ± 0.005613 (n.s.)** |
| final-depth ratio ON/OFF at **w40** (N94's undemoted floor) | **21.53 / 1.516 / 1.464 — geometric mean 3.628×, 3/3 seeds** (⛔ w4: **2/3**, geo 1.421×) |
| memory-**deleted** margin | ⚠ **positive on 6/6 cell-seeds** — the block does better with the memory gone |

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐ **The transferable sentence is about incentives, not about gradients: an optimiser maintains a component in proportion to what that component pays it, and a memory the loss cannot read is a memory the loss will flatten.** ⇒ **the durable fix for erosion is to make the store useful, and the stop-gradient partition is a cheap insurance policy on top — not a substitute.** ⭐ **The insurance is genuinely cheap and genuinely exact:** it adds no parameter, no state byte and no forward change; off, it is bit-identical to a sha256 fingerprint taken *before* the code was written; on, it makes the store's gradient exactly zero while leaving every read-side channel alive. ⛔ **But two honest clauses travel with it forever.** *(i)* **The protection claim is `w40`-scoped** — 3/3 seeds at the 40-step write budget, only 2/3 at the demoted 4-step one. *(ii)* ⛔⛔ **"Protected" is not "useful": the memory is still a net cost against simply deleting it, on every cell and seed measured.** ⭐ *A shield around a component that does not pay for itself protects the deficit too.*

**Status: [⭐ the shield is MEASURED, not asserted — exact-zero in-system gradient probe on the full two-layer block, plus a pre-build sha256 fingerprint test; both green in the 1397/0 post-merge suite] · [⚖ the registered gate printed `FAILS_FLATTEN` at both budgets and is **SUPERSEDED, NOT OVERTURNED** — the failing leg is the BASELINE, and the Head re-scoped the gate (§A23.2) before the component shipped; ⛔ quote the verdict only with its re-scope attached] · [⛔ TOY (0.16 M), 3 paired seeds, N94-demoted at w4; seed variance dominates — untrained depth alone varies 5.4× across seeds and every SE is comparable to its mean] · [⛔ the residual-OFF arm is a **labelled DIAGNOSTIC corner**, never a claim cell] · [⛔ any depth aggregate states linear AND geometric — and these estimator corrections are **post-hoc, reporting-side only, never a re-scoring**] · [⛔⭐ the pre-registered CSF3 abort criterion is **SUSPENDED**: replayed on this exact arm it pauses 2 of 3 healthy seeds (first trip at step 25, min 4.12e-4, recovering 15.2×)].** **Nomenclature (Def-2):** the *depth* here is a fitted well depth of the potential $V_\theta$, **not** a mass; the trainable $M$ appears only as the read's inertial mass and its gradient is one of the channels deliberately left ALIVE; **spectral $\mu$ does not enter this record.** **Reading pointers:** Record 21 and its C2W6 update (the collapse this retires, in its censoring form), Record 22 (the ψ payload channel — the very channel whose removal produces the uselessness arm here), Record 24 (the gradient path this partition also closes), registry **N223 addendum / N225 / N226 / N227 / N229 / N230**, charter **§A22 / §A23.1 / §A23.2 / §A23.4**.

> **⭐⭐ Update (C2W6 fold-2, 2026-08-06 — `c2w6-erosion-adjudication` §3.1/§4/§5/§6; `doc-curator-c2w6-fold-2`). FOUR CORRECTIONS TO THIS RECORD. ⛔ The record body above is not rewritten, and no verdict in it moves.**
>
> **1. ⛔⛔ The clause *"at the configuration we now ship, the catastrophic collapse simply does not happen"* is SUPERSEDED.** ✅ **It happens and then reverses.** The depth curve has a **transient trough**: **0.9 %** of untrained at step **150** and **5.3 %** at step **275** on 2/3 seeds (112× and 19× losses vs 1.05–1.33× reading noise), recovering by **geo 4.20×**. ⭐⭐ **The banked collapse was measured at 200 steps — inside that trough — so the E1 equation displayed above had its DENOMINATOR in the trough and was measuring RECOVERY.** ⇒ ✅ **say *"N223's mechanism FIRES at the run-2 config but is NOT TERMINAL"***; ⛔⛔ **never *"the premise is retired"*, never *"there is no erosion"*, and never the wave's own earlier replacement *"the monotone collapse does not reproduce."* ⛔ E1 remains REFUTED 0/3 — raw AND after netting the designed decay.**
> ⭐ **The teaching point this adds to the record, and it is the durable one: an endpoint RATIO is two chances to be wrong, and a ratio whose denominator is a fixed early reading is coupled to whatever the curve was doing at that step.** ⭐ **Design rule: fix the reference reading OUTSIDE the transient — `final ÷ max over the run`, or a registered late-window baseline.**
>
> **2. ⚠ The depth curve plotted here is RAW — it is not netted of the store's DESIGNED decay, and that decay's exponent drifts.** Every live slot is multiplied by `exp(−leak)` per chunk tick (`leak = 0.02`), and depth ∝ amplitude² ⇒ a per-tick relative drop of **1 − exp(−0.04) = 0.039211**. ⭐ **The law is exact — measured median 0.039211 (min 0.039210, max 0.039211) over 717 readings.** But a slot's `last_write_chunk` **drifts 0→12 within a run**, so each reading carries a different designed factor. **Netting it out moves the E1 seed-0 figure 9.78× → 6.47× (−34 %)**: ⭐ **up to a third of the apparent "recovery" is the allocator drifting toward later write chunks, not depth being restored.** ⛔ **Direction is conservative — netting makes the arm look MORE eroded, never less — so no verdict changes.** **Netting is a build requirement for the next wave that measures erosion.**
>
> **3. ⛔ The shield does NOT prevent the transient.** The partition-ON arm shows the same shape (min/untrained **geo 0.105**, troughs at steps 150 / 725 / 375) ⇒ ⭐ **the surviving depth loss is not carried by the partitioned channel alone.** ⚠ **And the `w40` protection figure in the table above is a SIGN statement, not a resolved magnitude: geo 3.628× **[1.49, 8.84] = 1.45 SE**, sign test 3/3 with one-sided p = 0.125.** ⛔ **"w40-scoped, 3/3" is exactly right and must not be upgraded to a magnitude.** ✅ **One clean positive to record alongside: the re-price trigger on the partition did NOT fire — partition-ON is below partition-OFF on only 1 of 3 seeds — so closing the write's gradient did not starve the write of its one useful signal.**
>
> **4. ✅ What is UNCHANGED and is the record's real content.** ⭐⭐ ***Erosion is a symptom of uselessness*** survives intact and is the **estimator-robust** claim in the wave: the residual-OFF corner erodes **significantly** (geo **0.0775×**, log-mean/SE **−3.10**) while the run-2 arm does not (geo 0.327×, −1.29 SE, n.s.) — a **4.2×** separation. ⛔ **Also unchanged: the memory is still a NET COST (memory-deleted margin positive 6/6), "protected" is still not "useful", and the abort criterion is still SUSPENDED** — ⭐ **and the measured 112× trough that recovers is now the strongest justification for that suspension, since it is exactly the false positive the rule would have fired on.**

> **⭐ Update (C2W10, 2026-08-11 — `c2w10-i2-usage-erosion`; `doc-curator-c2w10-fold`). THE USAGE-VS-EROSION QUESTION THIS RECORD OPENS WAS RE-MEASURED AT THE LIVE-WELL COUNT THAT WAS SUPPOSED TO SETTLE IT — AND THE ANSWER IS THAT THE RIG COULD NOT ASK IT. ⛔ Nothing above is edited and no verdict in it moves.**
>
> **(1) ⚖ The registered re-measurement returned `INDETERMINATE`, and that was the pre-priced modal outcome.** Power met for the first time (`n_live_max` **64 / 64 / 64**, scored **57 / 60 / 57**, detectable |ρ| **0.272 / 0.265 / 0.272**); ⭐ the usage proxy is **reliable at this rig, unlike C2W6's** (`ICC(1,1)` of item-id-keyed `read_hits` = **+0.2797 / +0.4466 / +0.4665**, positive 3/3). `ρ(U, E_netted)` = **−0.2396 / −0.2834 / −0.3246** — negative on 3/3, in the same place as C2W6's — but the 2-SE **upper** bounds are **+0.028 / −0.026 / −0.065**, so the registered CONFIRM branch fires **0/3**; `ρ(U, depth_netted)` = **−0.036 / +0.103 / +0.020** ⇒ nothing lifts. ⛔⛔ **The depth-as-feature-importance caveat stays exactly where it is — and it now TRAVELS.**
> **(2) ⛔⛔ And the finding is not the arithmetic: THE MECHANISM WAS NEVER EXERCISED.** The rig runs **no outer objective and no optimizer step on store parameters**, and the cell never rewrites a live well ⇒ *"gradient magnitude ∝ contribution"* **cannot act there at all.** The deferral that sent the question here checked the **live-well count** and never the **channel** — the count was in fact adequate. ⇒ ⛔ **a DECLARED NOT-RUN at this rig, never a null**, re-homed to a rig with a live outer objective. ⭐ *This is the same lesson as the record above it, arriving from the other side: **erosion is about incentives, so a rig with no incentive gradient cannot measure erosion**, however many wells it has.*
> **(3) ⭐ What DID land is the instrument.** Netting is now a **build requirement** — every depth curve emitted **raw AND netted** — and it did its job so completely that **nothing was left**: median netted total log-drop **4e-7 nats** against **0.58–0.62 nats** raw, a ratio of **6.8e-7**, i.e. ~4 float32 ULP, with **54/58/57 wells showing netted INCREASES as well as decreases.** ⚠ **Raw curves are monotone non-increasing on every well of every seed here** (`n_items_with_any_raw_increase = 0/0/0`), so ⛔ **no C2W6-style trough exists in this rig to be mistaken for decay** — and none may be inferred from it either, because this is a different substrate. **Never-quote, added here: `ρ(U, depth_raw) = +0.18 / +0.22 / +0.19` is NOT positive evidence** (below the floor **and** age-confounded, `ρ(E_raw, age) = +0.74 / +0.94 / +0.85`); ⛔ **and the pooled-across-seeds estimator FLIPS SIGN** (pooled `ρ(U,depth) = −0.216`). Registry **N297 / N298**, ledger ⟲ **C2W10**, charter **Add.14 §A39.4 / §A40.1 / §A41.4**.

### Record 24 — **The door that was never welded shut** (a zero derivative severs one path, not every path)

> **The door that was never welded shut.** Our write does not use ordinary gradient descent inside its inner loop — it uses the *sign* of the gradient. That is deliberate, and it has a pleasing consequence: the sign function's derivative is zero almost everywhere, so no learning signal can travel backwards through the inner write into the encoder that produced the item. ⭐ **We wrote that consequence into the code's own documentation as a construction guarantee — "the trajectory read is the only channel to the encoder, by construction as well as by theorem."** ⛔⛔ **It is false at the configuration we actually run, and the measurement is unambiguous: 27 % of the encoder's first-layer gradient flows through the write.** The reason is one line of code that has nothing to do with the inner loop. To make the write land its atoms *near* the item rather than anywhere, we assign the item's own coordinates into the atom centres — `centers[:, :addr] = z[:addr] + jig`. ⭐ **That assignment is a plain differentiable path from the encoder into the store's state, sitting entirely outside the sign-gated loop.** The sign function guards the door; the placement step walks in through the window beside it.

**The same thing in numbers** (`c2w6-anti-erosion` §2, the K1 in-system gradient probe on the full two-layer block at the run-2 config, seed 0, one batch; `atom_place_radius = 0.3`):

| leaf | partition OFF (L0 / L1) | partition ON (L0 / L1) | reading |
|---|---|---|---|
| atom `centers` / `log_width` / `amp` | 1.97e-03 / 1.67e-03 / **9.35e-03** … | **0.0 / 0.0** (all, both layers) | severed exactly |
| **`phi`** | **9.083e-02** / 2.768e-02 | **6.593e-02** / **4.350e-02** | ⭐ **−27 % at layer 0; layer 1 RISES** |
| loss | 5.764082908630371 | 5.764082908630371 | bit-identical ⇒ backward-only |

**Why the guarantee fails, in two lines.** Through the inner write the claim is exactly right — $\partial\,\mathrm{sign}(u)/\partial u = 0$ almost everywhere, so

$$\frac{\partial\,\text{store}}{\partial \varphi}\Big|_{\text{inner loop}} \;=\; 0 .$$

But the placement assignment adds a second term that the sign never touches:

$$\text{centers}[:, :\!\text{addr}] \;=\; z[:\!\text{addr}] + \text{jig}, \qquad z = \varphi(x) \quad\Longrightarrow\quad \frac{\partial\,\text{centers}}{\partial \varphi} \;=\; \frac{\partial z}{\partial \varphi} \;\neq\; 0 \quad\text{whenever } \texttt{atom\_place\_radius} > 0 .$$

⛔ **And `atom_place_radius = 0.3` is the run-1 / run-2 / run-3 configuration** — i.e. the guarantee holds in exactly the setting we stopped running.

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐ **The transferable lesson is about how architectural guarantees rot: the docstring was true when it was written and became false when a *different* feature was added, because nothing in the codebase connected the two.** A zero derivative severs *the path it sits on* — it says nothing about paths added later. ⭐ **The general practice this argues for is the one the wave used to catch it: probe every leaf's gradient in-system, on the full block, at the configuration you ship — not on the sub-module where the guarantee was proved.** ⛔ **And the immediate consequence is a program-wide quotation rule: every *"the write cannot train φ"* statement now carries the placement qualifier** — *"severed by sign-SGD only when `atom_place_radius = 0`; in the shipped run-1/2/3 config the localized-placement path carries 27 % of φ's layer-0 gradient, closed by `erosion_partition`."* ⚠ **What is NOT affected, and this matters because the two claims travel together in our documents: the settled-point statements** — $\partial q^*/\partial q_0 = 0$ and the bitwise-zero $\gamma$/$M$ gradients — **are about the READ and are untouched by any of this** (Records 15/18; N135/N152/N201).

**Status: [⭐ measured in-system on the full two-layer block, with the forward bit-identical — this is a backward-only reading] · [⛔ TOY (0.16 M), seed 0, ONE batch, layer-0 figure: the 27 % is a single-cell gradient-norm ratio, not a multi-seed statistic] · [⛔ it says the channel EXISTS where our documents said it could not; it does **not** say the channel helps or harms — closing it costs no bpc at the 2-SE bar, which is a null over 1000 toy steps, not a pricing] · [⚠ the layers are not independent: cutting layer 0's write path RAISES layer 1's φ gradient 0.0277 → 0.0435] · [✅ owner assigned — the engineer corrects `write_sign`'s docstring; ⚠ **the per-site sweep of the claim across docs and tracked code is `BLOCKED-PENDING` its grep list and no site has been edited**].** **Nomenclature (Def-2):** $\varphi$ is the read-**in** encoder (token → item), not a potential; the *sign* here is `jnp.sign` inside the inner write, not a spin variable; **neither inertial $M$ nor spectral $\mu$ enters this record.** **Reading pointers:** §11.4 (the gradient dichotomy — which channels reach which parameters, and why the trajectory one exists), Record 18 and its C2W5 update (H1b placement, the feature that opened this path), Record 23 (the partition that closes it), registry **N228 / N221**, charter **§A22 / §A23.3**.

> **⭐⭐ Update (C2W6 fold-2, 2026-08-06 — `c2w6-phi-leak-docstring`; charter ADDENDUM 9 §A27.3, Advisor-registered; `doc-curator-c2w6-fold-2`). THE SWEEP IS DONE, AND IT LEAVES BEHIND A STANDING RULE THAT IS MORE IMPORTANT THAN THE SWEEP.**
>
> **1. ✅ The `BLOCKED-PENDING` status in this record's Status line is DISCHARGED.** The engineer corrected the claim at **6 in-code sites** (comments and docstrings only — **zero behavioural change**, and the pre-build sha256 fingerprint still reproduces bit-for-bit, which is the strongest available evidence that nothing moved), and **pinned the mechanism with a regression test so the docstring cannot drift back**: at the run-2 rig, radius 0.3 with the partition **off** ⇒ `‖dL/dz‖` = **9.36e-04** (the leak is live) · same config with the partition **on** ⇒ **exactly 0.0, bitwise** · radius **0.0** ⇒ **exactly 0.0, bitwise**. ⭐ **A plain-SGD control at radius 0 reads 6.83e-09 — so the sign write's severance is genuinely real *at radius 0*, and placement re-opens the channel by roughly five orders of magnitude regardless of it.** ✅ **Doc side: the three highest-value targets (the "trains end-to-end only through the trajectory read" design rule, the `0.0 / 2.654e-9 / 6.421e-3` reference triple quoted as an end-to-end statement, and `settled_point` called the "zero-gradient control") occur ONLY in tracked code — zero occurrences in any program document.**
>
> **2. ⭐⭐ THE STANDING CONSEQUENCE, which is doctrine and not a detail of this wave.**
> - ⛔⛔ **`settled_point` is a zero-gradient CONTROL only at `atom_place_radius = 0`.** At radius > 0 the settled-point arm's gradient into φ is a **non-zero floor** — so the pre-registered "control that must measure ~0" is only a control in the regime where placement is off.
> - ⛔⛔ **Every banked `gradient_probe` trajectory/point ratio measured at the shipped run-1/2/3 config therefore has a non-zero DENOMINATOR, which means the published ratio UNDERSTATES the read's share.** ⭐⭐ **Say the direction whenever this is quoted, because it is the part that matters: the bias is CONSERVATIVE, never an inflation.** ⇒ ✅ **No claim built on those ratios is inflated. NOTHING is retracted. The ratios are LOWER BOUNDS on how dominant the read channel is.** ⚠ **The in-flight cluster run's own gradient-probe outputs inherit this, so the qualifier must travel with those artifacts when they land.**
> - ⛔⛔ **THE BOUNDARY, stated here so a future reader cannot over-apply the qualifier: the settled-point BITWISE ZEROS — $\partial q^*/\partial q_0 = 0$ and the exactly-zero $\gamma$/$M$ gradients (N135 / N152 / N201) — were measured at `atom_place_radius = 0` and are NOT AFFECTED. Do not widen the qualifier onto them.** ⚠ **Only the φ-channel rider that travels beside them needs it.** *(This is the same "what is NOT affected" clause the record body already carries, now with its reason: those measurements were taken in the regime where the guarantee holds.)*

## 11.23 Two pictures from C2W7: **the read that learned to count** and **the ruler that erased its own reading** **[plain-language records — new C2W7, filed 2026-08-06]**

**Why this section exists — and the one sentence to read before the two records.** For three waves the tier-ii story was *"the read cannot express the answer."* C2W7 built a read that can, and cleared the expressivity bar for the first time in the programme — ⛔⛔ **and the store still scored *below* the same head's own launches, on 5 of 5 seeds.** ⭐ **What the wave bought is therefore not a win but a relocation: the blocker moved from the read to the write, with a measured mechanism attached.** ⛔⛔ **Scope, binding on both records (charter §A28.4): this vehicle has the launch head, learned initial momentum and per-particle mass/friction and NOTHING else — no readout network at all, no consolidation or trash region, no traversal, no persistent store — and it runs in a factored-store gym, not inside a model. Nothing here is a verdict on the full system.** ⚠ **And the missing components are NOT the explanation: on this family the landscape is written identically for every query, so the settle cannot beat its own launches by construction.** Registry **N232–N240**; charter **ADDENDUM 8 §A24–§A26**, **ADDENDUM 9 §A27.2/§A28.4**; ledger ⟲ C2W7.

### Record 25 — **The read that learned to count** (a memory can be taught to say *how many*, and it still loses to its own front door)

> **The read that learned to count.** Record 20's read failed because it could not say *how many* rooms the answer contained: it fired four probes, they landed in about two rooms, and nothing in the machinery ever asserted "the answer has exactly four parts." ⭐ **So this wave gave the read a counting code.** The launch head now does two things at once: it **estimates the cardinality** of the answer — a single number `F_hat` — and it **allocates its twelve probe particles** across the rooms it believes are involved. The read then divides the particles by the estimate, and asserts every room whose share reaches one. ⭐⭐ **It works.** The read commits to **4.54** rooms where the previous one asserted a smudge of 5.79 that was never four; it names *exactly* the right number on **39 %** of queries; and the fraction of queries where the asserted set is **exactly right** goes from **0.0023 to 0.0656** — clearing, for the first time in the programme, a bar that was pre-registered before the harness existed. ⛔⛔ **And then the wave measured the only comparison that matters, and it is brutal: delete the landscape entirely — keep the same trained head, the same particles, the same reader, just remove the physics the particles roll through — and the answer gets BETTER.** Not by much, but on **5 seeds out of 5**, and by more than two standard errors: **−0.0070 ± 0.0016.** ⭐ **Worse for the story and better as science: the head that was never trained at all beats the trained one** (0.0695 vs 0.0539). ⇒ ***The read can finally say the answer. The store subtracts value from the head's own launches while it says it.***

**The same thing in numbers** (`c2w7-read-cardinality` §3/§4/§4.1/§4.2; Hub and Advisor re-derivations from `main/tierii_cardinality_summary.json`; 5 seeds, 1 280 unseen queries/arm, `N_a = 32` wells, `F = 4`, `k = 12` particles ledgered on every arm, `tol = 0.2338`, chance = 0.0000, stores **75 448 B** identical on every arm):

| gate leg | measured | bar | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| **`R1`** exact-set occupancy (gated, physics) | **0.065625 ± 0.023876** ⇒ mean − 2 SE **0.041749** | > 0.02 | ✅ **CLEARS** (iteration 1: **0.0023** — **28×**) |
| **`G1`** read − live launder (worst reader) | ⛔ **−0.007031 ± 0.001562**, per-seed −0.0078/−0.0078/−0.0039/−0.0078/−0.0078 | > 0 | ⛔ **FIRES, 5/5 seeds** |
| **`S_eff`** | 16.7737 ± 0.8527 — **30.6 of 32 wells visited** | *(band retired — Record 26 / N239)* | ⚠ **instrument artifact** |
| **`M15`** launch collapse | 0 trips; `S_marg` 23.158 ± 2.655 | ≥ 16 | ✅ quiet |

| the mechanism | launch (launder) | after the settle |
|---|---|---|
| distinct wells occupied | **4.834 ± 0.125** | ⛔ **5.668 ± 0.396** |
| occupancy precision (raw) | **0.4578 ± 0.0260** | ⛔ **0.4107 ± 0.0191** |

**Why it fails, in one line of arithmetic.** The read asserts room *j* when its share reaches one,

$$m_j \;=\; \hat F\cdot\frac{c_j}{\sum_{j'} c_{j'}},\qquad \text{assert}\;\{\,j : m_j \ge 0.5\,\},$$

so a clean allocation of twelve particles over four rooms (`3/3/3/3`) gives every $m_j = 1$ — **and a settle that scatters one particle per room into a neighbour turns it into `3/2/3/2/2`, where $m_j$ lands on neither 1 nor 0.** ⭐ **The scattering is not a bug in the head: the store's attractors sit 1.09 — about 1.4 well-spacings — from its designed anchors, so a particle launched at anchor *j* can legitimately fall into the basin next door.** ⛔ **And there is a second, deeper reason the launder cannot be beaten here: on this family every well is written on every query, so the landscape carries no query-specific information at all — the settle cannot add what the launch did not already contain, by construction.**

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐ **The transferable lesson is about which component a null belongs to.** Three waves of evidence said *"the read is the problem"*; this wave fixed the read, cleared its bar, and the deficit did not move — ⇒ **the blocker was never only the read, and now it has an address: the write's geometry.** The named next lever is mechanical and its success criterion is already measured: **make the write objective place attractors AT the designed anchors** (scatter 5.67 → ≈4.8, and the sign of `G1`). ⭐ **Two smaller lessons ride along, both worth stealing.** *(i)* ⛔ **A capacity dial that is really a resolution dial:** doubling the particles at **exactly 2× the ledgered read flops** moves the score and coverage by **0.0000** — *once the head commits to a cardinality, more particles only refine where the committed rooms are.* *(ii)* ⭐ **A monitor built before the fix it authorises, and exercised by accident:** the launch-collapse monitor stayed quiet on the claim cell (so the regularizer stayed off, and turning it on moved nothing) — **and tripped un-forced on a real training run at `S_marg ≈ 7.9`.** ⭐⭐ **And the statistic's level was load-bearing: per-query perplexity 3.94 (≈ `F`, the design working) against a marginal of 21.22 — a regularizer penalising per-query concentration would have attacked the mechanism it was built to protect.**

**Status: [⭐ measured, 5 seeds, every gate leg re-derived from raw independently by the Hub AND the Advisor, digit-for-digit; jax 0.9.0, float32; merged `main @ fdab86d`, suite 1397/0 post-merge] · [⛔⛔ `R1` / the `F`-commitment / *"the cardinality mechanism works"* is NEVER quotable without `G1` in the same paragraph] · [⚖ the gate fails on `G1` ALONE as substance; the `S_eff` leg is retired as an instrument artifact; NO organizer swap was run and NO `OD_min`/F1/F2/F5 number exists — the tier-ii verdict is UNREACHED for a third wave, ⛔ **NOT refuted**, and the honest-null path does not fire] · [⛔⛔ VEHICLE-SCOPED: no ψ at all (grep-verified), no consolidation/trash, no traversal, no persistent store, gym rig not the streaming block ⇒ no full-CLU verdict exists; ⚠ and the missing components are NOT the deepest defect and must never be offered as the explanation] · [⛔ TOY: one claim cell, one γ pair, `d = 8` only; the out-of-class ceiling 0.691 is quoted only with its `(d, draws)` noise model] · [⛔ the read track is PAUSED — there is no third read iteration and none is scoped].** **Nomenclature (Def-2):** the per-particle **inertial $M$** and friction $\gamma$ are the read's dynamics (`kinetic_mode = newtonian_learned`, per-particle `mass_override`); $\hat F$ is a **cardinality estimate**, not a mass; **spectral $\mu$ does not enter this record.** **Reading pointers:** Record 20 and its C2W7 update (the read this replaces), Record 21 and its C2W7 update (the settle, whose failure changes sign here), Record 26 (the reader that had been erasing this kind of signal), registry **N232 / N233 / N234 / N235 / N236 / N240**, charter **§A24 / §A25 / §A26.1 / §A26.5 / §A26.6 / §A28.4**.

### Record 26 — **The ruler that erased its own reading** (fit your reader to the wrong loss and it will score a live signal at exactly zero)

> **The ruler that erased its own reading.** To turn a memory's settled state into an answer you need a *reader* — a small map from the physics's output to the value you wanted. We fit those readers by least squares, because that is what one does. ⭐ **And the metric we score them by is not least squares: it is all-or-nothing.** A prediction counts only if it lands inside a tolerance; miss by a hair and the query scores zero. ⛔⛔ **Those two facts do not compose.** Least squares, faced with a signal it cannot fully explain, does the sensible thing for *its own* loss: it **shrinks** — it multiplies its output by about 0.4, pulling everything toward the mean, because a timid prediction has smaller squared error. **And a timid prediction is a wrong answer under a threshold.** On the very cell of Record 25 this was worth a factor of **28**: a 72-parameter fitted reader scored **exactly 0.0000** on a latent where a reader with **zero parameters** — literally *"read the payload as it is"* — scored **0.0539**. ⇒ ⛔⛔ **A reader can score an informative memory at exactly zero, and nothing in the pipeline warns you.** ⭐⭐ **Which raised the question that mattered more than the wave itself: how many of this programme's published zeros were scored through such a reader?** The answer required no new science — the latents were already banked — and it came back in a day: **the earlier zeros do not move.** ⭐ **And the reason they do not move is the most useful thing the episode produced: the artifact's size is bounded by ONE number you can compute before you fit anything at all — the fraction of queries whose asserted answer is *already exactly right*.** At the new cell that fraction was **~18 %**, so the artifact could be worth 28×. At the older cells it was **2 of 2560, 3 of 1280, and 0 of 2560** — so the artifact was worth **two or three queries**, and there was nothing for it to destroy.

**The same thing in numbers** (`reader-fitting-audit` §1/§3/§4/§5/§6; three banked cells × 5 seeds, re-scored with **zero-parameter identity readers ADDED to** the published class, never substituted; ⭐ **three reproduction gates passed bit-for-bit with the EXISTING readers before a single identity number was looked at**):

| cell | best fitted | best identity | $I - F$ | ⭐ **`K6`: queries already exactly right** | `tol` crossing fires? |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `orgdiv-null-arms` N1 @ selected | 0.0000 | **0.0000 ± 0.0000** | **+0.00000** | **0 / 2560** | no — *nothing to destroy* |
| `orgdiv-null-arms` N3 @ grid-max | 0.00078 | 0.00117 | +0.00039 | **2 / 2560** | ⭐ **YES (seed 2)** |
| `tierii-read-fix` physics | 0.00078 | **0.00234** | **+0.00156** | **3 / 1280** | ⭐ **YES (seed 3)** |
| `orgdiv-cat-test` physics | **0.00078** | 0.0000 | ⛔ **−0.00078** *(identity is WORSE)* | **0 / 2560** | no |
| *(C2W7's own cell, for reference)* | *0.0000* | ***0.0539*** | ***+0.0539*** | ***~18 %*** | *YES* |

**Where the crossing fires, the mechanism is exact:** the fitted reader's residual on the *already-correct* queries is **2.47×** and **2.91×** the tolerance, where the identity reader's is **0.0000** — it keeps **3 of 3** and **2 of 2** correct queries, the fitted reader keeps **none**.

**Why the bound exists, in two lines.** Payloads live on a sphere of radius $R$ and the tolerance is a quarter of the payload RMS, so **substituting a single wrong well costs**

$$\|v_a - v_b\| \;\approx\; \sqrt{2}\,R \;=\; 2.96\!-\!3.02 \times \texttt{tol},$$

i.e. **any set that is not exactly right is already far outside tolerance.** ⇒ ⭐⭐ **a zero-parameter reader's score IS the asserted set's exact-set accuracy, to within one query** — which makes that accuracy the **closed-form ceiling** of every zero-parameter reader and the **exact headroom** of any fitting artifact. **This was derived in the pre-registration and then held to the last digit: the identity reader's score came back per-seed `[0, 0, 0, 0.01171875, 0]`, bit-identical to the published `R1`.**

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐ **The transferable rule is one sentence: a metric and its fitting criterion must agree, or the fit is an adversary.** ⭐ **And the practice that falls out of it costs nothing and is now mandatory here in two parts.** *(i)* **`K6` — before any reader is fitted, report the fraction of queries whose asserted answer is already exactly right.** No store, no fit, no reader; **18 % means an artifact can be worth 28×, 0.1 % means it is worth two queries.** *(ii)* **Every reader class carries a zero-parameter member — added, never substituted, never reported alone.** ⚠ **The "never substituted" clause is not pedantry, it is measured: the parameter-free twin can be strictly WORSE** (+0.0109 on a real cell; **> 0.99** on a designed control where the payload table is rescaled and the fitted reader absorbs the scale exactly while the identity reader dies) — **so replacing the class with its free member would itself be a laundering step.** ⛔ **And a warning in the opposite direction, from the same audit: least squares against a threshold is unstable BOTH ways — on one cell it inflated (`diag(W) = +46.5`, mean residual 13.48 against the identity's 1.84) ⇒ a fitted reader's residual is never evidence that a store is "close" to the answer.**

**Status: [⭐⭐ VERDICT: SURVIVES — `null*` = 0.001171875 unmoved, `OD_min` −0.00078125 identical, N1 1.0000/0.0000 unmoved, largest identity-minus-fitted gain anywhere +0.00234 (21× below the 0.05 bar, 4.3× below the pre-registered 0.01 PARTIAL threshold, 2 SE straddling 0 everywhere); merged `main @ d70898b`, suite 1410/0 with HEAD verified unmoved] · [⭐ three reproduction gates bit-for-bit BEFORE any identity number was looked at, two of them on non-zero seed-asymmetric published values] · [⛔⛔ "SURVIVES" is NEVER "the readers are fine" — it means *the fitting artifact cannot have produced these particular zeros*; the artifact is real and it fired twice] · [⚠ the honest qualifier travels: the tie is a tie AT ≈ 0 — 2, 3 and 0 exactly-right queries — so it rules out a reader artifact, it does not make any arm alive] · [⛔⛔ the hope that the artifact hid a physics dividend is MEASURED AND REFUTED: adding the free reader makes iteration 1's `G1` WORSE, −0.0016 → −0.0023] · [✅ Add.5 §A20 stands as written; NO erratum is owed; `PREREG-TierII.md` untouched and no `ERRATA-TierII.md` exists] · [⛔ the sentence that started this — *"every arm this programme has scored at ≈ 0 may have been scored through a reader that destroys its own signal"* — is MEASURED FALSE for the earlier cells and is a never-quote; the binding form is the proportionality above].** **Nomenclature (Def-2):** `diag(W)` is a fitted reader's **gain matrix**, not a mass or a width; `tol` is a metric tolerance, not a certificate radius; **neither inertial $M$ nor spectral $\mu$ enters this record.** **Reading pointers:** Record 25 (the read whose signal this class was erasing), Record 20 (the cells that were re-scored, and their `null*`), §11.12 (why one number cannot score a controller — the same "report both, always" instinct), registry **N212 footnote / N215 addendum / N237 / N238**, charter **§A26.3 / §A27.2 / §A28.3**.

## 11.24 One picture from READ ITERATION 1 (`tierii-read-fix`) — **the map and the ground** **[plain-language record, filed RETROACTIVELY 2026-08-06 by the catch-up fold; the wave sits between C2W5 and C2W7]**

> ⚠ **Why this record is dated after the records that cite it.** Read iteration 1 was never folded into any transfer document, so its numbers reached Records 20/21/25/26 as citations without a home. This section gives them one, ⭐ **written in its already-corrected form** — every statement below already carries the C2W7 corrections (`K0` = reachability only · `S_eff` 16.00 = *all 32 wells visited* · `k` = a resolution dial, not capacity). ⛔ **No read iteration 3 exists or is implied (§A26.6).**

### Record 27 — **The map and the ground** (the addressing was repaired; the store's wells are not where the design says they are)

> **The map and the ground.** Record 20's read failed for a reason anyone can state: four probes landed in 2.2 rooms when the answer needed 4. So the next iteration fixed exactly that — **twelve probes instead of four, a wider address space, a launch head that pushes them apart** — and the fix worked on its own terms, completely. ⭐ **The probability of reaching at least the four rooms the answer needs goes from 0.05 to 1.00; the probes occupy 11.8 distinct rooms; and the settle, which used to cost 23 % of that occupancy, now costs 4.6 % — and turns into a *gain* of +0.076 once you keep only the probes that actually rolled downhill.** ⛔⛔ **And the answer did not get any better.** The number of queries where the read names *exactly* the right set is **0.0023 ± 0.0047** against a bar of 0.02 — one seed carried it and four scored zero — every arm, every reader and the control tie within a single query of zero, and, worst of all, **the full physics read scores below the same head's own launches with the landscape deleted (−0.0016 ± 0.0019)**. ⇒ ⛔ ***Being able to reach the right rooms is not the same as being able to say which ones, and the store was adding nothing to its own front door.*** ⭐⭐ **Then the same wave, probing its own store for an unrelated reason, found the thing that would explain the next two waves: it descended 512 probes into the landscape to see where the wells actually are — and they are not where the design put them.** The attractors it found sit **1.09 away from the nearest designed anchor — about 1.4 well-spacings — and 476 of the 508 "minima" it found have depth exactly 0.0000: they are resting points in the confinement bowl, not wells at all.** ⇒ ⭐ ***The map and the ground disagree.*** A launch aimed at anchor *j* can therefore land in a neighbour's basin — which is precisely why, one wave later, a read that commits to "three here, three there" gets back "three, two, three, two, two" (Record 25).

**The same thing in numbers** (`tierii-read-fix` §1/§3/§3.1/§4/§7/§10; 5 seeds, 1 280 scored unseen queries/arm, `N_a/F/K` = 32/4/128, `a/m/d` = 32/8/8, `k` = 12, `depth_ratio` = 3.0, read budget **400 + 800** Verlet at `dt = 0.05`, `γ` 0.05/0.02, `tol` = 0.2338, chance = 0.0000, bar = chance + 0.05, stores **75 436 B** identical on both arms; jax 0.9.0, float32):

| quantity | refuted `P = 4` read | **this read** |
|---|---|---|
| `K0` = $P(\ge F$ distinct wells reachable$)$, bar 0.90 | **0.0477** | ⭐ **1.0000** |
| distinct wells, launch → settled | 2.202 → **1.70** | **11.8156** → **11.2688 ± 0.5189** |
| the settle's cost in distinct wells | ⛔ **−23 %** | **−4.6 %** |
| occupancy precision, settled + descent-gated | *(no gate existed)* | ⭐ **0.3502 ± 0.0193** (**+0.076** over its own launch) |
| ⛔ **exact-set occupancy (`R1`, the answer)** | **0.0000 / 2 560** | ⛔ **0.0023 ± 0.0047** (bar 0.02 — **INDETERMINATE**, 1 of 5 seeds) |
| ⛔ **`G1` = read − the same head's own launches** | — | ⛔ **−0.0016 ± 0.0019** |
| organizer swap, `OD_min` | — | **−0.0008 ± 0.0016** — ⛔ **a VACUOUS tie** |
| ⭐ store probing: candidates / kept / distance to nearest designed anchor | — | **508.4 of 512** / **32.0** / ⭐ **1.0871 ≈ 1.4 `sep`**, with **476 of 508 at depth 0.0000** |

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐ **Three transferable lessons, and the third is the one the programme is now acting on.** **(1) Name the component, then fix *that* component — and expect the next failure to be a different one.** Addressing was the diagnosed defect, addressing was repaired, and the score did not move: ⛔ *"`K0` repaired, `S_eff` in band, the settle's destruction reversed"* are all true and all statements about **reaching**, never about **answering** — ⭐ **the report registered that never-quote about itself, and it is binding: they never appear without `R1` and `G1` in the same paragraph.** **(2) A precondition that cannot fail tells you nothing.** `K0` is a **reachability** statement: one wave later a collapsed head scored `K0 = 1.000` while every reader read 0.0000. ⇒ ⛔ **reachability is a precondition, never a health check.** **(3) ⭐⭐ Check whether your store's wells are where your design thinks they are — it is a 512-probe descent and it costs nothing.** That single measurement is the mechanism behind C2W7's scattering result, and it converts *"the read is the problem"* into ***the write is the problem***: the next iteration's first lever is to make the write objective place the attractors **at** the designed anchors, and its success criterion is already measured on both ends.

the RELATIVE predictions survived] · [⛔⛔ VEHICLE-SCOPED (§A28.4): a factored-store gym rig, not the streaming block; no consolidation/trash in the claim cell, no traversal, no persistent store — ⛔ no full-CLU verdict may be stated or implied, and the missing components must never be offered as the explanation] · [⚠ ψ was SHIPPED on every arm and no ψ A/B was run (Advisor amendment A2, no mid-task hot-swap) — ψ's marginal value is NOT measured here] · [⛔ ~~"`S_eff` = 16.00, in band"~~ → **"all 32 wells visited"**: the band's lower half is unreachable by construction and 16 was the only attainable value (§A26.4)] · [⛔ ~~"doubling `k` raises the score" (+0.2383 coverage)~~ → REFUTED at a committing head (0.0000 at exactly 2× ledgered flops);
