it is not a benchmark result].** **Nomenclature (Def-2):** the head learns **per-particle inertial $M$** and frictions ($\gamma_{\rm address} = 0.05$, $\gamma_{\rm read} = 0.02$); **spectral $\mu$ does not enter**; `sep` is a well spacing (0.7773) and `s` a well width (0.2879, confinement-subtracted), never a mass. **Reading pointers:** Record 20 (the read this one replaced, and `K0`'s origin), Record 21 (the settle that merges — ⚠ at this head the sign flips, see Record 25), Record 25 (the cardinality commitment that came next), Record 26 (the reader audit that re-scored this exact cell), registry **N241–N249** (and their cross-links **N232–N239**), charter **§A20.3(b)–(f)**, corrections **§A24 / §A26.4 / §A26.6 / §A27.2 / §A28.4**.

## 11.25 Three pictures from C2W8 **PASS 1**: **the gate that could not fail**, **the wells nobody can knock on**, and **the better the address, the less the physics adds** **[plain-language records — new C2W8 pass 1, filed 2026-08-06]**

**Why this section exists — and the two sentences to read before the three records.** C2W8's job was the **well lifecycle**: over-dig freely, merge to budget, prune what is useless, and route the pruned wells into a trash region built in C1 and never used. ⭐ **The wave built the *census* first — the instrument that decides whether the lifecycle has anything to act on — and the census killed the stage it was gating, for reasons nobody had predicted.** ⛔⛔ **Scope, binding on all three records: this is PASS 1 only.** A second pass (three engineer spokes) was in flight and had **measured nothing** when these records were written; ⛔ **nothing here is a pass-2 result.** ⛔ **Every census reading is labelled non-promotable** (the census had to run at address dimension 8; Record 29 says why), and ⛔ **no full-CLU verdict, no tier-ii verdict and no I2 verdict exists in this wave.** Registry **N250–N261**; ledger ⟲ **C2W8 pass-1**; sources `c2w8-well-lifecycle` (+ `ERRATA-C2W8.md`, `census.json`) and `c2w8-cifar-strong-phi`, merged at `main @ 80d7d4b`, suite **1443/0** with HEAD verified unmoved across the run.

### Record 28 — **The gate that could not fail** (a criterion whose two halves were both unable to say "no", and the label that described the wrong mechanism)

> **The gate that could not fail.** Before building the machinery that merges and prunes wells, the wave did the disciplined thing: it wrote down, in advance, a **census** that decides whether such machinery has anything to act on. Two quantities. **`P`** — how many live, working wells are never read (those are the ones worth pruning). **`M`** — how many pairs of wells are near-duplicates (those are the ones worth merging). If both are tiny, the lifecycle is building verbs with nothing to do, and the wave stops and publishes that. ⭐ **The census ran, computed `stage2_unlock = true` by its own arithmetic, and the honest answer is that neither number measured what it was registered to measure.** ⛔ **`P` came back at 0.02 — below its own threshold — while 37 of 48 wells were in fact never read (77 %, an order of magnitude above the bar).** Why? Because a well only counts as a "live attractor" if a probe dropped nearby rolls back into it, and on two seeds of three **not a single well in the store captured anything at all**: every capture radius was exactly zero. The test's second half was worse — the depth floor is *defined* as "the deepest well among those that failed to capture", so **when nothing captures, the floor becomes the deepest well in the store and no well can exceed it.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **two independent halves of the same criterion, both forced false by construction: `P = 0` was a tautology, not a measurement.** **And `M`?** Every admitted pair had payload distance exactly zero, and the geometric half — *are the two wells actually close together?* — used a certificate radius **10–11× larger than the spacing between keys**, so it **admitted every pair and refused nothing.** ⇒ **`M` was measuring "a class-incremental stream contains two items of the same class", which is true by construction.** ⭐⭐ **The wave's saving grace is the thing it was designed around: this surfaced AT THE CENSUS, before a single verb was built on it** — and the engineer shipped only the one component that passed its own designed negatives and **declared the rest cut, with the reason.**

**The same thing in numbers** (`c2w8-well-lifecycle` §3/§3.2 + `census.json`; Hub re-derived both legs from raw independently of the report; 3 seeds, `addr_dim = 8`, `overdig = 2.00`, capacity 16 / well budget 8, `σ_q = 0.15`):

| leg | measured | threshold | what it actually measured |
|---|---|---|---|
| **`P`** live attractors never read | **0.020833** (per-seed **0.0625 / 0.0000 / 0.0000**) | > 0.05 to unlock | ⛔ **a tautology** — 1 of 48 wells passes `is_attractor`; **36 of the 37 never-read wells are excluded SOLELY by the capture test** |
| **`M`** mergeable near-duplicate pairs | **0.238889** (28/120 · 29/120 · 29/120) | > 0.05 to unlock | ⛔ **the same-class pair rate, exactly** — `R_cert` **1.5402/1.4177/1.6482** = **10.9×/10.3×/11.2×** the key spacing (0.1407/0.1375/0.1468); max admitted separation 1.271 |
| **`stage2_unlock`** | **true** (`kill = false`) | — | ⚖ **mechanically correct, licensed by neither leg** |

**The arithmetic that makes `P` unsatisfiable**, in one line. With `θ_att` defined as the largest depth among the wells that did **not** capture,

$$\texttt{is\_attractor}(i) \;=\; \big[\lambda_{\min}(z_i) > 0\big]\;\wedge\;\big[\,\text{depth}_i > \theta_{\rm att}\,\big]\;\wedge\;\big[\,r_{\rm cap}(i) \ge \sigma_q\,\big],$$

so if **no** well captures, then $\theta_{\rm att} = \max_i \text{depth}_i$ and the middle conjunct is false for **every** well — while the third is false by assumption. **Two conjuncts, one cause, zero survivors.**

**⛔⛔ And the label that has to change everywhere.** The census files 15–16 wells per seed as `population_eroded_not_attractor`. **Those wells carry depth 0.4–2.2 — not depth → 0.** The registered erosion mechanic says erosion drives depth *to zero*, so that an eroded well **ceases to exist**; these wells exist, have positive curvature, and fail only the capture test. ⇒ ⭐ **they are wells that EXIST but are UNADDRESSABLE at the query scale — the reach condition (`s ≳ σ_q`), a different mechanism with a different fix.** ⛔ **No artifact may cite this population as erosion evidence** (N251).

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐ **The transferable rule is the one C2W7 learned about a band and this wave learned about a gate: check what your instrument CAN POSSIBLY SAY, not only what it said** — and do it *when you register it*, not when it returns a number you like. ⭐ **The cheap version, and it is now the standing requirement here: any replacement criterion must state, BEFORE it runs, what value each of its conjuncts takes on the shipped rig.** ⚠ **The second lesson is about labels, not arithmetic:** a population named after the mechanism you expected will be *cited* as evidence for that mechanism, by someone who never opens the depths. **Name a population after what was measured, not after what it was predicted to be.**

**Status: [⭐ measured, 3 seeds, one operating point; `census.json` computed the unlock MECHANICALLY, and the engineer did not override it — the scoping consequence is the Hub's] · [⛔⛔ K1 must be RE-REGISTERED before any stage 2 exists; stage 2 §2.2–2.7 is DECLARED CUT WITH CAUSE, ratified — ⛔ never a null, never a budget excuse] · [⛔ the registered prediction N1 is FALSIFIED (`P` predicted 0.15–0.45, measured 0.0208); N2 is numerically confirmed but DEGENERATELY] · [⛔⛔ `population_eroded_not_attractor` is a PROGRAM-WIDE relabel: it is the reach condition, not erosion] · [⛔ TOY, non-promotable (`phi_dim = 8`), `γ_φ` OFF in every cell, no claim cell was run] · [⚠ the defect originates in the wave's own pre-registration and the Hub filed it against itself].** **Nomenclature (Def-2):** `θ_att` is a **depth floor** and `r_cap` a **capture radius** — neither is a mass; `λ_min` is the smallest eigenvalue of the Hessian at the **relaxed** site (slot ≠ well); **neither inertial $M$ nor spectral $\mu$ enters this record.** **Reading pointers:** Record 29 (why the wells cannot be reached, and why the store is inert one dimension up), Record 27 (the same *"the map and the ground disagree"* failure on the tier-ii rig), Record 26 / §11.24 (`K6`, the precondition-before-the-mechanism pattern this census is an instance of), registry **N250 / N251 / N252 / N254**, charter **§A21 / Add.9 §A28.3**.

> **Update (C2W8 pass 2, 2026-08-09).** ⭐⭐ **This record is now instance 1 of a named defect class with THREE members in one wave, and the class has a name: *A GATE THAT CANNOT FAIL ON THE THING THAT MATTERS*.** **(1)** the vacuous `M` described above; **(2)** ⛔ **monitor #3's admission gate, whose refusal rate is `0.000` — it has never refused anything, and it STILL trips on 3/3 seeds on both pass-2 arms** (untouched, and correctly *not* silently fixed); **(3)** ⛔⛔ **pass 2's own capture gate, which passed all three of its legs on all three seeds on an arm where NO read landed in any basin at all** (`n_never_read = 16/16` every seed, launder margin **−0.594**) — because the three legs measure retrievability **at the sites** and none of them asks whether a query **reaches** its site. ⭐⭐ **Instance 3 was caught by its own spoke, against its own interest, in its first ten lines.** ⇒ ⭐ **the rule this record states ("check what your instrument CAN POSSIBLY SAY") gains its companion: check whether the CONJUNCTION can say "no" to the failure you actually care about.** ✅ **Partly discharged since:** the merge criterion is **re-registered as K9** — `rho_geom = 1.0` against the **measured** key spacing (commensurability **1.00×**, against the 10.9×/10.3×/11.2× above), an **anti-vacuity clause** that REFUSES a degenerate payload channel (this record's exact configuration), a clause that **refuses rather than certifies when the ruler is unmeasured**, 19 tests, and **every pair pass 1 admitted now refused on geometry, 3/3.** ⛔ **The `P` leg, the K1 gate itself and the merge VERB are NOT discharged** (`test_no_merge_verb_exists` asserts the verb was deliberately *not* built). ⚖ **Head ruling: the missing addressability leg, G-ADDR, is registered before anything else is scored.** Registry **N250** (amended) **/ N263 / N273**; charter **Add.10 §A29.3 / §A30.1**.

### Record 29 — **The wells nobody can knock on** (a store can dig real wells, be exactly local in its parameters, and still be unreadable — because atoms have tails)

> **The wells nobody can knock on.** The store's job is to carve a dip in a landscape for each item, so that a noisy query dropped nearby rolls into it. ⭐ **This wave measured, for the first time on this rig, all three parts of that sentence separately — and they come apart.** **The dips are real:** median well depth rises from 0.7 to 1.7 over the stream, and the curvature at every single well is positive (the landscape genuinely bends upward in all directions, 0.79 to 8.87). **The dips cannot be knocked on:** the measured capture radius — how far you can displace a probe and still have it return — is **exactly zero on 47 of the 48 wells.** ⇒ ⛔ **positive curvature is NECESSARY and NOT SUFFICIENT for a basin**, and the store re-reads its own items **at chance** (0.0625 out of 0.0625). ⭐⭐ **Then the diagnosis, and it is the wave's best sentence.** Each write is allowed to touch only its own block of atoms, and it obeys that **exactly**: over 105 write events per seed, the amount an item's *own* wells lose is **precisely** the designed decay, to float32 — **zero violations.** But **78–84 % of writes RAISE the contribution of FOREIGN atoms at another item's site**, and on **45 of 48 wells the foreign contribution exceeds the item's own.** ⇒ ⭐⭐ ***A write touches only its own atom block — but atoms have TAILS. Local in parameters is not local in the landscape.*** That is also why depth *rises* while retrieval *falls*: the "depth" at an item's site is mostly **not that item's well**. ⭐ **And one dimension up, the tails win completely:** at the address dimension the continual-learning entry actually requires (16), the nearest of *all* 131 072 atoms sits **1.48** away while the atom width is **0.3**, so the write's gradient carries `exp(−r²/2s²) ≈ 5e-6` and **the store digs nothing at all.** ⛔ **And it is not starved: the atom budget scaled exactly as designed at every dimension.** *The constraint is not how many atoms you buy; it is how far each one reaches.*

**The same thing in numbers** (`c2w8-well-lifecycle` §3.1/§3.3/§6 + `ERRATA-C2W8` §3/§5, filed **before** the cells; 3 seeds; C2W6's residual instrument **imported, not edited**):

| what was measured | result |
|---|---|
| curvature `λ_min` at the relaxed site | **> 0 everywhere, 0.791 … 8.873** |
| ⛔ measured capture radius | **exactly 0.000 on 47 of 48 wells** (one capturer, r = 0.508) |
| ⛔ self-probe `decode` | **0.0625 = chance 0.0625**, 3/3 seeds (`acq` 0.086 / 0.117 / 0.078) |
| site drift (relaxed vs recorded site) vs key spacing | **0.216 … 1.473** vs **0.14** |
| ✅ C3 locality **in parameter space** | **0 violations**, max residual **0.0**; own-leg drop **0.019801 = 1 − e^{−0.02}, exact** |
| ⛔ C3 locality **in function space** | **`rate_up_foreign` 0.838 / 0.781 / 0.819**; foreign > own on **45/48** (median own **0.518/0.282/0.123** vs foreign **1.261/0.947/0.611**) |
| depth, raw → decay-netted (B1) | 1.659 → **1.898** (+14.4 %) · 1.239 → **1.490** (+20.2 %) · 0.725 → **0.832** (+14.8 %) |

**Why the store dies at `d = 16`, in one line of arithmetic.** Atom centres are drawn $c_j \sim \mathcal{N}(0, I_{\dim})$ with $\dim = d+1$, so the nearest of *all* atoms to a unit-norm site recedes as $\sim\!\sqrt{\dim}$ while the atom width stays $s = 0.3$; the write's gradient carries

$$\exp\!\Big(\!-\frac{r^2}{2 s^2}\Big):\quad d=4:\ 0.294 \to 6.18\mathrm{e}{-1} \;\cdot\; d=8:\ 0.738 \to 4.86\mathrm{e}{-2} \;\cdot\; d=12:\ 1.252 \to 1.65\mathrm{e}{-4} \;\cdot\; \mathbf{d=16:\ 1.483 \to 4.98\mathrm{e}{-6}},$$

and the measured depth after three designed-site writes follows it down: **0.48–0.60 → 0.34–0.46 → 0.30–0.35 (one seed already 0.000) → 2.1e-9 / 6.8e-10 / 0.000.** ⭐ **The atom budget was co-scaled exactly as specified (`round(512·√2^d)` = 2 048 / 8 192 / 32 768 / **131 072**, verified against the shipped code) — buying atoms geometrically does NOT bring the nearest one closer.**

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐ **Three transferable statements, and the middle one is the wave's.** **(1) Curvature is not a basin.** A memory that is provably curved at every stored point can still be unaddressable at the noise scale its own queries are drawn at — ⛔ **so "the landscape has a minimum there" is never evidence that the item can be retrieved.** **(2) ⭐⭐ Locality has two meanings and only one of them was ever checked.** Parameter locality (which weights a write may touch) is a *safety* property and it held **exactly**; function locality (what the landscape looks like elsewhere afterwards) is the property retrieval actually needs, and it fails four times in five. ⭐ **The same lesson, one level up, is what made this wave's one shipped component correct: the trash region's damping gate had to be COMPACT — exactly zero beyond its radius — because a sigmoid tail makes a "local" change global.** ⛔ **A tail is a global edit with a local name, whether it belongs to a gate or to an atom.** **(3) When capacity and reach are confused, you buy the wrong thing.** The fix indicated here is **reach** — better launches, traversal — **not more atoms**; and the honest ledger entry is that *`d = 16` costs 131 072 atoms at this rig's scale and still digs nothing.*

**Status: [⭐ measured, 3 seeds; ERRATA §3 filed BEFORE the cells it governs; B1 decay-netting pytest-asserted exact against `exp(−leak·n)` at `rel = 1e-4`] · [⛔⛔ the `d ≥ 16` inertness carries its provenance wherever quoted: the arithmetic came from a SCRATCH PROBE of 3 designed-site writes, not a censused CL-stream cell; the confirmation cell is a CANCELLED, DECLARED NOT-RUN — ⛔ never a null] · [⛔ the census therefore ran at `addr_dim = 8` and every reading is `flags.promotable = false` — a RIG limitation, ⛔ never K1's kill (Record 28)] · [⚠ `own/foreign` is a DIAGNOSTIC, never a target and never a gate leg: under private wells a high foreign share is interference, in a FACTORED store it is the signal — the invariant that survives both designs is RETRIEVABILITY] · [⛔ TOY, one rig, 105 events/seed; no claim cell, no full-CLU verdict] · [⚠ `atom_local_radius` cannot rescue a φ-addressed stream — it needs per-group targets at INIT, and a stream does not know its addresses until write time].** **Nomenclature (Def-2):** `s` = **atom width** (0.3) and $\sigma_q$ = **query jitter** (0.15) — neither is a mass; "depth" is a **fitted well depth**, ⛔ **never quotable as feature importance** (§A23.5 ACTIVE); **neither inertial $M$ nor spectral $\mu$ enters this record.** **Reading pointers:** Record 28 (the gate this instrument's zeros made unsatisfiable), Record 27 / **N244** (attractors 1.4 spacings from their designed anchors — the same failure family on the tier-ii rig), Record 23 (erosion — ⛔ **and the population C2W8 relabels is NOT an instance of it**), registry **N252 / N253 / N254 / N261**, charter **§A21 / §A26.6 / Add.9 §A28.3**.

> **Update (C2W8 pass 2, 2026-08-09) — this record's central FACT was repaired and its central CAUSE was corrected; both changes are in place, and nothing above is deleted.** **(1) ✅ THE WELLS CAN NOW BE KNOCKED ON.** Bounding **how far each atom's influence reaches** — a width **co-scaled to the measured key spacing** plus **site-local atom init at admission** — turns *"capture radius exactly 0.000 on 47 of 48"* into **basins on 46 of 48**, median radius **0.4297 / 0.2930 / 0.3633 = 2.0–2.9 σ_q**, self-probe `decode` from **exactly chance** to **+4.02 / +4.75 / +3.65 SE**, site drift from 0.216–1.473 to **0.00100 / 0.00652 / 0.00150** — **at IDENTICAL BYTES** (360 960; the kernel changed, not the budget). ⛔ **But it is NOT compactness:** a plain **Gaussian** at the same co-scaled width also clears the gate 3/3 and **decodes better** (+6.21/+4.75/+6.94 SE), while compact support only sharpens capture and drift ⇒ ⛔ **never *"compact atoms made the store capture"*; the registered lever is *co-scaled width + site-local init*, i.e. bounded reach.** **(2) 🔴 THE CAUSE SENTENCE IS CORRECTED.** ✅ **The measurement above stands exactly** (parameter locality exact, 0 violations; `rate_up_foreign` 0.838/0.781/0.819; foreign>own 45/48). 🔴 ***"atoms have TAILS"* is no longer a complete account of WHY**: a write that **places** atoms instead of dragging them gives **foreign > own on 0/48** with foreign median **0.0012–0.0152** against **0.611–1.261** ⇒ ⭐⭐ ***a 300-step gradient write must MOVE an item's atoms across the ball to dig, and every item's travelling atoms are every other item's foreign background.*** **Right about the symptom, wrong about the cause.** **(3) ⛔⛔ AND THE THING THIS RECORD IS *ABOUT* DID NOT MOVE:** reads landing in **no basin 58 / 62 / 62 of 64 — digit-identical to pass 1**, items never read **15/15/14 of 16** (pass 1: 14/14/14), read-vs-launder margin **−0.354** (pass 1: −0.333). ⇒ ⭐ ***the wells became real and the queries still don't reach them*** — the root cause being **encoder geometry** (`σ_q = 0.15` against a key spacing of 0.138–0.147, so a noisy query is as far from its own key as its key is from its neighbour, and the launder itself degrades 1.00 → 0.31 across the stream). **(4) ⚠ THE INSTRUMENT.** K7 confirms this record's zeros were a **measurement**, not an instrument failure (a planted basin at 0.30 reads 0.30859; a planted flat site reads **exactly 0.0**) — ⚠ **but the reading has a FLOOR of `tol/expansion-rate` and a FALSE POSITIVE of ≈`r_hi` for a flat site at the confinement minimum**, so a positive radius is only ever read beside `λ_min` and the radius magnitude. **(5) 🔴 ONE CLAUSE ABOVE IS REFUTED:** *"`atom_local_radius` cannot rescue a φ-addressed stream — it needs per-group targets at INIT"* is **wrong in its strong form** — **write time is exactly when the address IS known**, and applying the localization **at admission** is both usable and **load-bearing** (without it the compact arm is *exactly* dead: depth 0.000, capture 0/16, decode at chance). ⚖ **Ruled compliant with the anti-pin prohibition, with a counterfactual check attached.** ⛔ **Unchanged: nothing at `d ≥ 12` was measured, and the `d = 16` arithmetic keeps its scratch-probe provenance.** Registry **N262 / N264 / N265 / N266 / N267**, and **N252 / N253 / N254** as amended; charter **Add.10 §A29.1–§A29.5 / §A30.3**.

Nothing above is deleted.** **(1) ⛔⛔ NEVER-QUOTE the pass-2 update's root-cause clause** — ~~*"the root cause being encoder geometry (σ_q = 0.15 against a key spacing of 0.138–0.147, so a noisy query is as far from its own key as its key is from its neighbour)"*~~. **`median_nn_task1` is the spacing of a ~200-key SIZING set; the store holds 16 items.** Against the real store population **σ_q/spacing = 0.32 (MNIST) and 0.19–0.37 (CIFAR)** ⇒ ⭐ **queries are ~3× CLOSER to their own key than to a neighbour — the OPPOSITE of the retracted sentence.** ⛔ **The failure is real and reproduces; its CAUSE IS OPEN**, registered as a theorist-priced question (**N275**). ⭐ **Same defect, two other victims: monitor #3's 0.000 refusal rate was ARITHMETIC (`d_safe` sized on 200 keys, applied to 16 items), and pass 3's cue difficulty was arm-dependent by 30 %.** **(2) ✅ THE `d ≥ 16` INERTNESS IS NOW A CENSUSED MEASUREMENT, so this record's scratch-probe provenance caveat RETIRES for `d = 16`:** at a **fully honoured** budget, `d = 8` (**8 192** atoms) median fitted depth **0.1479** · `d = 12` (**32 768**) **0.0186** · ⛔ **`d = 16` (131 072) 5.44e-7 = INERT, 0/6 usable wells** (**N283**). ⚠ **And the qualifier that must now travel with the reach story: *"not inert" is NOT "healthy"* — only 3/6 (at `d = 8`) and 2/6 (at `d = 12`) designed writes produced a usable well.** ⛔ **`d = 16` is a DECLARED NOT-RUN for the spine, never a null**; ⭐ **`d = 12` is the operational reach ceiling and is now charter substrate (Add.12 §A34.10).** **(3) ✅ AND THIS RECORD'S CENTRAL SENTENCE SURVIVES BOTH ERRATA:** the wells were real and the reads were not arriving — but ⛔ **the *evidence* pass 2 used for the second half is retired too** (see Record 31's pass-3 update): the honest statement is that **the reads reach the correct well about half the time and lose to nearest-key indexing on every protocol tested.** Registry **N274 / N275 / N283**, charter **Add.11 §A31.1–§A31.2**.

### Record 30 — **The better the address, the less the physics adds** (fix the feature space and the null re-prices — and the settle becomes literally nearest-neighbour lookup)

> **The better the address, the less the physics adds.** A year of this program's continual-learning work has one recurring embarrassment: replace the store with the most trivial competent substitute — a nearest-neighbour lookup over the very same keys — and the substitute ties or wins. w25 had measured a **null** on Split-CIFAR-10 and diagnosed the cause as the *address space*: the features were PCA of raw pixels, and PCA of CIFAR is nearly useless. ⭐ **So this wave built three real feature extractors, trained without labels on task-1 classes only, froze them, and re-ran the whole benchmark.** **The diagnosis was right:** accuracy goes **0.161 → 0.213 → 0.267 → 0.319** across the arm ladder, a **+0.159** lift with **zero** change to the store or the stream discipline. ⛔⛔ **And the same features raise the trivial substitute by exactly as much.** The store still finishes **0.014 below** its own nearest-neighbour launder, on **0 of 5 seeds positive**. ⭐⭐ **Then the decomposition that makes this the sharpest laundering statement the program holds.** Compare the store not to a *better-managed* buffer but to **its own keys** — the same memory, the settle deleted, plain `argmin‖φ(q) − key‖`. At the PCA arm the settle was **0.048 worse** than that. **At every strong arm it is a tie to four decimal places: +0.0003, −0.0007, +0.0002.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ ***In a good address space the damped settle IS nearest-key indexing.*** The entire remaining 0.014 deficit is bought by something with no physics in it at all: **the control buffer's class-balanced admission policy.** ⭐ *The good news and the bad news are the same measurement — the feature space really was the null's cause, and in the better feature space the store's own contribution is 0.000 ± 0.0004.*

**The same thing in numbers** (`c2w8-cifar-strong-phi` §1–§5; Split-CIFAR-10 **reduced** protocol, Class-IL, `task1_only`, 200 items matched everywhere, ⭐ **the stream is bit-identical across arms at a given seed (SHA-256 verified) so lifts are PAIRED**; φ params **ledgered on every arm including both launders**):

| arm | CLU ACC | its **same-keys** kNN | ring-buffer kNN | **CLU − same keys** | BWT |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `pca`-32 (the banked-null reference, 3 seeds) | 0.161 ± 0.004 | 0.209 ± 0.009 | 0.226 ± 0.005 | ⛔ **−0.0480 ± 0.0050** | −0.144 |
| `randconv` (architecture only, sees no data, 5 seeds) | 0.213 ± 0.006 | 0.213 ± 0.007 | 0.235 ± 0.005 | **+0.0003 ± 0.0003** | −0.222 |
| `convae` (reconstruction, 3 seeds) | 0.267 ± 0.006 | 0.268 ± 0.005 | 0.277 ± 0.003 | **−0.0007 ± 0.0007** | −0.235 |
| ⭐ **`simclr` (contrastive, 5 seeds)** | **0.319 ± 0.005** | 0.319 ± 0.005 | **0.333 ± 0.006** | **+0.0002 ± 0.0004** | ⛔ **−0.243** |

**Placement, with the byte ledger** (total floats): CLU **278 536** (store 53 000 + **φ 225 536**) · replay field ER / iCaRL / GDumb **695 890** ⇒ **2.50× cheaper**; iCaRL **0.419** (−0.100 above CLU), **level with GDumb 0.318 ± 0.029**, **+0.159** over the best rehearsal-free baseline. ⚠ **The strong φ is 4.25× the store's own memory — any "N floats per item" sentence about CIFAR that omits the encoder is wrong by a factor of five.**

**Why the settle cannot beat the lookup here, geometrically.** Measured on all four arms at end of stream: the corrected packing slack is **0.331–0.341**, i.e. $\sigma_q \approx$ median-NN — *a test query is about as far from its nearest address as two addresses are from each other.* ⇒ **with basins that crowded, the settle can only land in the nearest well**, and ⛔ **a better φ does NOT de-crowd the store: crowding is intrinsic to a CLASSIFICATION stream** (the query is a different image, not a corrupted copy).

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐ **The transferable rule: a re-price SCOPES a null, it does not retire it.** The honest sentence — and the only form these numbers travel in — is *"Split-CIFAR was a null at frozen-PCA φ; re-priced at strong φ it reads 0.319, still 0.014 below its own kNN launder."* ⛔ **"The encoder improved CLU" is wrong: the encoder improved the ADDRESS SPACE, and it improved the launder by the same amount.** ⭐ **The second lesson is a gift, because it converts a mystery into a cheap, named question:** the residual deficit is **not** the read — it is **admission**. *Why does our controller's own class-balanced eviction rule under-perform a plain balanced ring buffer by 0.014?* ⭐ **That is the only visible route to crossing the launder on this benchmark, and it needs no φ compute.** ⚠ **And the third is a warning about a number we already hold:** the store's CIFAR forgetting advantage **shrinks as φ improves** (BWT −0.144 → −0.243) — ⭐ **w25's *"the store does not forget; it never knew"* is now literally quantified** — so ⛔ **a forgetting advantage measured at the broken φ may not be carried to a strong one without re-running it.**

## 11.26 Three pictures from C2W8 **PASS 2**: **the wells nobody can find**, **the lever that was not the one we named**, and **the exam that cannot ask the question** **[plain-language records — new C2W8 pass 2, filed 2026-08-09]**

**Why this section exists — and the two sentences to read before the three records.** Pass 1 left the programme with a store that digs real wells nobody can knock on. Pass 2's directive was blunt: **make the store capture** — by controlling **how far each atom's influence reaches**, ⛔ **and explicitly NOT by pinning attractors to designed anchors** (that would make the settled point a deterministic function of item identity, which is the table-expressible configuration the programme's own intervention doc prohibits). ⭐ **Two arms ran it two different ways, both succeeded completely, and the wave's most valuable products are the three corrections its own spokes filed against their own results.** ⛔⛔ **Scope, binding on all three records: PASS 2 only.** Pass 3 (the strong-φ spine) had **measured nothing** when these were written; ⛔ **nothing here is a pass-3 result**, and pass-3 rulings appear only as *dispositions*. ⛔ **The arm A vs arm B race is VOID as a comparison and UNADJUDICATED — no record below scores it.** ⛔ **Every reading is non-promotable** (address dimension 8), **`γ_φ` is OFF**, **no claim cell ran**, and ⛔ **no full-CLU, tier-ii or I2 verdict exists.** Registry **N262–N273**; ledger ⟲ **C2W8 pass-2**; sources `c2w8p2-{compact-atoms, emission-head, instruments-and-debt}` (+ `ERRATA-C2W8-PASS2.md`, raw `capture_armA.json`) and charter **Add.10 §A29–§A30**, merged at **`main @ 1eda6a0`**, suite **1495/0** with HEAD verified unmoved across the run.

### Record 31 — **The wells nobody can find** (we repaired the memory's basins completely, and the number of queries that land in one did not change by a single digit)

> **The wells nobody can find.** A memory of this kind works in two steps: **carve a dip for each item**, and **have a query roll into the right dip.** Pass 1 measured that the dips were real and that **nothing rolled into them**. Pass 2 fixed the first half, on both arms, on every seed, and by a mechanism you can state in one sentence: ⭐ **stop letting each atom's influence spill across the whole space — set its width from the *measured* spacing between keys, and start its atoms near the site that is about to be written.** The result is emphatic. **Basins go from 1 of 48 to 46 of 48.** The distance you can displace a probe and still fall back is now **two to three times the query noise**, where before it was **exactly zero**. The store re-reads its own items **well above chance** (3.6–4.8 standard errors) where before it read **exactly at chance on every seed**. The settled point sits **0.001–0.007** from the recorded site against a key spacing of **0.14** — a hundredfold margin. And all of it at **exactly the same number of bytes**: the kernel changed, the budget did not. ⛔⛔ **And now the other half, which is the reason this record exists.** Count how many reads, launched from the item's own address, land in **any** basin at all. Pass 1: **58, 62, 62 out of 64 landed in none.** Pass 2, after all of the above: ⛔ **58, 62, 62 out of 64 — the same three digits.** Items never read once: **15, 15, 14 of 16**, against pass 1's 14, 14, 14 — *marginally worse.* The gap to a trivial nearest-neighbour lookup over the same keys: **−0.354**, against pass 1's **−0.333**. ⇒ ⭐⭐ ***The wells are real. The queries still don't reach them.*** ⚠ **And the reason is not the physics: the query noise (0.15) is the same size as the distance between neighbouring keys (0.138–0.147)** — a noisy query is as far from its own address as its address is from its nearest neighbour — **so at this operating point the items are not separable by ANY read**, and the trivial lookup itself decays from 1.00 to 0.31 across the stream.

**The same thing in numbers** (arm A, `capture_armA.json`, 3 seeds; the frozen pass-1 census re-run **unmodified**; the addressability rows are the **Advisor's**, computed from the raw cells because neither arm's report foregrounded them):

| what was measured | pass 1 | **pass 2 (arm A)** |
|---|---|---|
| **G-CAP** — wells with a non-zero capture radius | **1 / 48** | ⭐ **46 / 48** (16/16 · 15/16 · 15/16) |
| — median capture radius | 0.000 | **0.4297 / 0.2930 / 0.3633** = **2.0–2.9 σ_q** |
| **G-DEC** — self-probe `decode` (chance 0.0625, SE 0.0214) | **exactly at chance, 3/3** | **0.1484 / 0.1641 / 0.1406** = **+4.02 / +4.75 / +3.65 SE** |
| **G-DRIFT** — median site drift vs key spacing ≈ 0.14 | 0.665 – 0.925 | **0.00100 / 0.00652 / 0.00150** |
| curvature `λ_min` at the relaxed site | 0.791 – 8.873 | **9.85 – 55.75** |
| own contribution beats foreign at its own site | 3 / 48 | **44 / 48** |
| bytes (`clu_total_bytes` / launder, matched items) | 360 960 / 288 | **360 960 / 288 — identical; the arm buys ZERO** |
| ⛔ **reads landing in NO basin** | **58 / 62 / 62 of 64** | ⛔⛔ **58 / 62 / 62 of 64 — digit-identical** |
| ⛔ **items never read** | 14 / 14 / 14 of 16 | ⛔ **15 / 15 / 14 of 16** |
| ⛔ **read-vs-launder margin** | −0.333 | ⛔ **−0.354** |

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐ **The transferable statement is a separation, and the programme did not have it before: *a memory can be made retrievable AT ITS OWN SITES without becoming addressable FROM ITS OWN QUERIES, and the two properties move independently.*** ⛔ **So "the store captures" is never a synonym for "the store works", and the two halves above are one fact that is never quoted apart.** ⭐ **The second lesson is where the bottleneck actually is:** the failure that survives is a property of **the address space**, not of the landscape — which is why the wave's next step is the same census at a **strong φ**, and why the pass-1 CIFAR result (0.161 → 0.319 by changing only the encoder) is the same finding seen from the other side. ⚠ **And a warning that has to travel with the prettiest number here:** *drift → 0* means the settled point is becoming a **deterministic function of the stored key** — the table-expressible configuration the programme forbids — ⛔ **so no gate leg may ever treat drift → 0 as a target**, and the compliance check attached to the mechanism is a counterfactual: **can the attractor move off the stored key when the write objective demands it?**

**Status: [⭐ measured, 3 seeds, `gate_pass = true` computed MECHANICALLY, the census instrument re-run unmodified, K7 (the capture instrument's own two-sided validation) green FIRST] · [⛔⛔ the two halves are INSEPARABLE — a "the gate passed" sentence without the addressability half is non-compliant] · [⛔ the addressability numbers are the ADVISOR's, computed from raw; neither arm's report foregrounded them] · [⛔ TOY, one operating point (`addr_dim = 8`, `overdig = 2.00`), non-promotable, `γ_φ` OFF, no claim cell, no performance claim, the gate is BYTE-BLIND by ruling and no number is quoted at the 1 253× ratio] · [⚠ the gate passing is NOT retrieval: `decode` 0.141–0.164 is far from 1.0 — the store has basins and holds its sites; it is not yet a good memory] · [⚠⚠ drift → 0 is the D2a/table-expressible configuration and is NEVER a target; the counterfactual check rides on pass 3].** **Nomenclature (Def-2):** `s` = **atom width** (co-scaled: 0.211 / 0.206 / 0.220), `σ_q` = **query jitter** (0.15), `r_cap` = **capture radius**, `λ_min` = smallest Hessian eigenvalue at the **relaxed** site — ⛔ **none of these is a mass; neither inertial $M$ nor spectral $\mu$ enters this record** (both arms run `newtonian_learned` and neither varied a mass axis). **Reading pointers:** Record 29 **and its pass-2 update** (the pass-1 state this reverses, and the cause correction), Record 32 (which lever actually did it), Record 33 (why the gate could not have told us the second half), Record 30 (the same "φ is the binding constraint" finding on CIFAR), registry **N262 / N267**, charter **Add.10 §A29.1–§A29.2 / §A29.5–§A29.6 / §A30.2**.

Nothing above is deleted.** **(1) ⛔⛔ NEVER-QUOTE THE HEADLINE STATISTIC:** ~~*"58, 62, 62 out of 64 landed in none — the same three digits"*~~ and every quotation of it, **including this record's table row and the *"the same three digits"* sentence.** **Why:** `covered` is evaluated at the **LAUNCH POINT `q₀`** against the codebook (`clu_system._read_diagnostics`), so at fixed φ and a fixed admitted codebook **it is the same number whatever the store does** — ⭐ **the digit-identity that made this record's point was store-invariant BY CONSTRUCTION**, and `n_never_read` inherits it. ⭐ ***The general lesson, now standing doctrine: when a number is IDENTICAL across an intervention that should have moved it, the first hypothesis is not "the intervention failed" — it is "this quantity cannot see the intervention."*** **(2) ✅ WHAT REPLACES IT, measured on the SAME CELLS by the completed leg:** ⭐⭐ ***both arms address*** — arm A correct-basin **A1 = 0.50 / 0.42 / 0.52** against a threshold of **0.25**, arm B **0.93 / 0.98 / 0.88** — **and both still lose the launder leg** (launder **0.79–0.90** against a store at ≈0.5). ⇒ ⭐⭐ ***THE STORE CAN ADDRESS AND STILL LOSES TO A ZERO-PARAMETER READ OF ITS OWN KEYS.*** The settle-side never-addressed fraction on those same cells is **0.25–0.375** (arm A) and **0.0625–0.125** (arm B), **not 0.94/1.00.** ⚠ **The Hub's registered prior Q2 predicted A1 = 0.03–0.12 and is FALSIFIED UPWARD by a factor 4–5 — recorded as falsified, not dropped.** ⛔ **Pass 2's *"arm B never addresses"* is retired with the telemetry that produced it.** **(3) ⛔⛔ AND THE RECORD'S CLOSING CAUSE IS RETRACTED:** ~~*"the query noise (0.15) is the same size as the distance between neighbouring keys (0.138–0.147) … so at this operating point the items are not separable by ANY read"*~~ is **NEVER-QUOTE** — it divides an absolute noise by the spacing of a **~200-key SIZING set** while the store holds **16 items**; against the real population the ratio is **0.32 (MNIST) / 0.19–0.37 (CIFAR)**, i.e. **queries are ~3× CLOSER to their own key than to a neighbour.** ⛔ **The failure is REAL and reproduces; its CAUSE IS OPEN** — theorist-priced, consistent with the settle-destroys-launch-information family (Record 21, **N213**), **and explicitly not assumed.** **(4) ✅ UNTOUCHED BY BOTH ERRATA:** every capture-half number in the table above (basins 46/48, radius 2.0–2.9 σ_q, decode +3.65…+4.75 SE, drift 0.0010/0.0065/0.0015, own-beats-foreign 44/48, **identical bytes**) and the **−0.354** read-vs-launder margin, which G-ADDR **reproduced to the digit** (−0.359 / −0.500 / −0.203). ⚖ **And the compliance counterfactual this record's warning demanded RAN: `follow = 1.0079` at arm A's own kernel ⇒ the attractor CAN move off the stored key; capture was an OUTCOME, not pinning; the ruling STANDS** (Record 34). Registry **N274 / N275 / N266**, charter **Add.11 §A31.1–§A31.2**.

### Record 32 — **The lever that was not the one we named** (the wave's own 2×2 took its headline away, and the same 2×2 corrected a cause the programme had been carrying for a wave)

> **The lever that was not the one we named.** The pass-2 design hint came from the wave's own earlier lesson: a *"local"* change with a soft tail is not local at all, so **make the atom's influence exactly zero beyond a radius** — a **compact** kernel. The arm that did this cleared every gate leg. ⭐ **Then it ran the 2×2 that separates its two ingredients, and the answer was not the one the arm is named after.** Compact kernel **without** the second ingredient — starting the atoms near the site about to be written — reproduces pass 1 **exactly**: nothing captures, the store re-reads at chance, the landscape at every well is the bare confinement bowl, fitted depth **0.000**. **And a plain old Gaussian *with* that second ingredient, at the same co-scaled width, clears all three legs on all three seeds — and re-reads its own items BETTER than the compact arm does.** ⇒ ⭐⭐ **the load-bearing lever is *the width co-scaled to the measured key spacing, plus atoms that start where the write is about to dig* — bounded reach. Compactness sharpens the basin and pins the site; it is not what made the store capture.** ⛔ **"Compact atoms made the store capture" is a sentence that would have shipped, and it is wrong.** ⭐⭐ **The second correction is bigger, and it points at the write.** Pass 1 explained the store's interference — every item's site dominated by *other* items' atoms — as an intrinsic consequence of atoms having tails. The other arm replaced the 300-step gradient write with a head that **places** the well in one forward pass, and the interference **vanished: foreign beats own on 0 wells of 48, against 45 of 48.** ⇒ ***a gradient write has to DRAG an item's atoms across the space in order to dig, and every item's travelling atoms are everyone else's background.*** **The old diagnosis was right about the symptom and wrong about the cause.**

**The same thing in numbers** (arm A §7/§7.1, census strength, same 3 seeds, same width — only the flags differ; and arm B §4.3):

| cell | capture > 0 | `decode` (SE above chance) | median drift | fitted depth | foreign > own |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **arm A** — compact **+** site-local init | **1.000 / 0.938 / 0.938** | 0.148 / 0.164 / 0.141 (**+4.02 / +4.75 / +3.65**) | **0.0010 / 0.0065 / 0.0015** | 0.606 / 0.683 / 0.581 | 4 / 48 |
| **AB1** — **gaussian** + site-local init | 0.875 / 0.750 / 0.813 | ⭐ **0.195 / 0.164 / 0.211 (+6.21 / +4.75 / +6.94)** | 0.0146 / 0.0412 / 0.0308 | — | 4 / 48 |
| **AB2** — compact, **no** site-local init | ⛔ **0 / 16** | ⛔ **0.0625 = chance** | 0.6307 | ⛔ **0.000** | 15 / 16 |
| *pass 1 (for reference)* | *1 / 48* | *exactly chance, 3/3* | *0.665–0.925* | *0.725–1.659* | *45 / 48* |

**And the cause correction, in one row** (arm B, a *placing* write at the same census): **foreign > own 0 / 48**, foreign median **0.0012 / 0.0152 / 0.0050** against pass 1's **1.261 / 0.947 / 0.611**, own median **1.321 / 1.333 / 1.326**.

**Why a compact atom is dead without the second lever, in one line.** An atom further than its support radius `R` from every sampled point has **identically zero** gradient with respect to its amplitude, centre and width — *it is inert permanently and can never be recruited.* With the shipped scattered initialisation the nearest own atom sits at **1.088** while a co-scaled support is **0.176**: ⇒ **0 of 512 own atoms in support, fitted depth exactly 0.000.** With site-local init the nearest own atom is at **0.0281** and all **512 / 512** are in support.

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐ **Two transferable rules, and they are about attribution rather than mechanism.** **(1) An arm that bundles two levers has no attribution until it runs the 2×2 — and the ablation must be registered *before* it runs**, because here the engineer's registered prediction was **falsified**, a single pilot seed then over-called the correction **in the other direction**, and only the census-strength re-run produced the form that survives. **(2) ⭐⭐ Before blaming a defect on a structural property of your representation, check whether your own *optimisation procedure* manufactures it.** The tails were real; most of the damage attributed to them was put there by a write that had to drag atoms to dig. ⛔ **An artifact of the procedure will imitate a property of the architecture, and the cheapest discriminator is a different write — not a better measurement of the same one.** ⭐ **Both corrections were published by the spokes that lost by them, in their first ten lines.**

**Status: [⭐ AB1 measured at CENSUS strength (3 seeds) after a one-seed pilot over-called it; both ablations registered with predictions in a dated erratum BEFORE running] · [⛔⛔ NEVER-QUOTE "compact atoms made the store capture" — the registered form is *co-scaled width + site-local atom init*, kernel form is a SECONDARY axis] · [⛔ the cause correction does NOT retract pass 1's measurement: parameter-space locality exact (0 violations) and `rate_up_foreign` 0.838/0.781/0.819 stand exactly as published — only the CAUSAL clause is superseded] · [⚠⚠ `own/foreign` is a DIAGNOSTIC, never a target and never a gate leg — under private wells a high foreign share is interference, in a FACTORED store it is the SIGNAL; the invariant that survives both designs is RETRIEVABILITY] · [⚠ the own/foreign estimator HARD-CODES the Gaussian kernel, so it is exact for AB1 and arm B and OVER-reads the compact arm — the two columns are not comparable] · [⚖ site-local init ruled COMPLIANT with the anti-pin prohibition — it sets where atoms START, not where the attractor ENDS — with a mechanical counterfactual check attached to pass 3] · [⛔ TOY, one operating point, non-promotable, no claim cell].** **Nomenclature (Def-2):** `s` = **atom width**, `R = 2.5 s` = **support radius**, `α` = the confinement coefficient (`λ = 2α = 0.1000` is *the bare bowl*, i.e. **no well at all** at the relaxed point) — ⛔ **no mass of either kind enters this record.** **Reading pointers:** Record 31 (what the lever bought and what it did not), Record 29 + its pass-2 update (the cause this corrects), Record 23 (erosion — ⛔ still a different mechanism), registry **N264 / N265 / N266 / N271**, charter **Add.10 §A29.4 / §A30.3**.

### Record 33 — **The exam that cannot ask the question** (three gates in one wave that could not fail on the thing that mattered — and the one that was caught by the person it would have paid)

> **The exam that cannot ask the question.** A gate is supposed to be able to stop you. This wave produced **three** that could not — and the wave's real product is that it noticed all three. **The first** was pass 1's merge criterion: it asked *"are these two wells close together?"* using a radius **ten to eleven times** larger than the distance between neighbouring keys, against a population whose payload distances were **identically zero** — so it **refused nothing**, and *"mergeable pairs"* was measuring *"a class-incremental stream contains two items of the same class."* **The second** is an admission monitor whose refusal rate is **0.000** — it has never refused anything, in any wave, and it is **still open.** **The third is the interesting one.** Pass 2's capture gate has three legs: do wells have basins, does the store re-read its own items above chance, does the settled point sit near the recorded site. One arm passed **all three, on all three seeds** — and in the very same cells, **not one read launched from an item's own address landed in any basin at all** (16 of 16 items never read, on every seed), while a trivial nearest-neighbour lookup beat it by **0.594**. ⇒ ⭐⭐ **The three legs measure whether the memory is retrievable AT ITS OWN SITES. None of them asks whether a query REACHES its site — and that is the entire point of a memory.** ⭐⭐⭐ **And the arm that proved this is the arm it cost.** The spoke that passed the gate opened its own report with *"the pass-2 gate is blind to addressability-from-φ, and arm B is the proof"*, put the damaging numbers in its first ten lines, and recommended measuring the missing leg **on both arms, from data already banked, before anything was adjudicated.** ⚖ **The ruling that followed: the missing leg is registered before anything else is scored, and the comparison between the two arms is void until it exists.**

**The same thing in numbers** (arm B, `census_armB.json`, 3 seeds — every row from the *same* cells):

| the gate said | the same cells also said |
|---|---|
| **G-CAP** 0.938 / 1.000 / 0.875 ✅ | ⛔ **`n_never_read` = 16 / 16 on EVERY seed** — no read landed in any basin |
| **G-DEC** 0.2500 on all three, **8.8 SE** above chance ✅ | ⛔ read accuracy from `φ` **0.135** vs a kNN-in-φ launder **0.729** |
| **G-DRIFT** 0.0373 / 0.0305 / 0.0179 vs spacing 0.141 / 0.138 / 0.147 ✅ | ⛔ **margin −0.594** (pass 1: −0.333) |
| ⇒ **all three legs, all three seeds: PASS** | ⇒ ⛔⛔ **the store is retrievable from its own sites and not addressable from its own queries — and the gate cannot tell the difference** |

**Why the read never arrives, in one line of arithmetic.** The write objective required each launch's **nearest** well to be its own; **nothing required the well to lie inside the read's actual reach from `φ`.** At an emitted width `s ≈ 0.30`, a launch **1.0** away feels
$$\exp\!\Big(\!-\frac{1.0^2}{2\,(0.30)^2}\Big) \;=\; 4\times10^{-3}$$
of the well's force — **so the 400-step address phase never arrives.**

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐⭐ **The doctrine, now standing: *A GATE THAT CANNOT FAIL ON THE THING THAT MATTERS.*** The earlier version of this lesson (from the census that unlocked a stage it could not license) was *check what your instrument can possibly say, conjunct by conjunct*. This wave adds the harder half: ⭐ ***check whether the CONJUNCTION can say "no" to the failure you actually care about*** — three passing legs can coexist with total failure of the property the wave exists to move. ⛔ **The mechanical requirement: the missing leg is two-sided, carries designed negatives proving it CAN fail, and is pytest-asserted, before anything is scored on it.** ⭐⭐ **And the cultural point, which is the one to keep: the defect was found by the spoke that benefited from it, reported against its own interest, in the first ten lines — and the same spoke separately registered the label that bars its own configuration from making the programme's headline claim.** *That is what a healthy wave looks like from the inside: the strongest evidence against a result is produced by the person holding it.*

**Status: [⭐ measured, 3 seeds, both arms; the blindness is a property of the GATE, not of either arm — arm A is scored on the same legs and is blind the same way] · [⛔⛔ the arm A vs arm B race is VOID as a comparison and UNADJUDICATED; the race re-opens only on the completed gate] · [⛔ arm B is permanently CLAIM-BARRED by construction — one private well per item, one-hot coefficients ⇒ `NO_TIER_II_CLAIM`, machine-checkable and travelling with every number, the pre-registered P6 = 0.85 outcome] · [⛔ QUOTATION HOLD on arm B's G-DEC: `decode` is EXACTLY 0.2500 = 32/128 on all three seeds while its own acquisition varies (0.984/0.961/0.938) — that reads as STRUCTURAL, not measured; the mechanism must be explained first, and the census records no per-well decode so it is not decomposable] · [⚠ monitor #3's refusal rate 0.000 is STILL OPEN and still trips 3/3 on both arms — untouched, and correctly not silently fixed] · [⛔ TOY, non-promotable, no claim cell, no tier-ii / full-CLU / I2 verdict].** **Nomenclature (Def-2):** `s` = **emitted well width**, `ρ·s` = the reach hinge's slack, `σ_q` = **query jitter** — ⛔ **no mass of either kind enters this record**; "depth" here is a **fitted well depth** and is ⛔ **never quotable as feature importance** (§A23.5 ACTIVE). **Reading pointers:** Record 28 **and its pass-2 update** (instance 1 of the same defect class, and the criterion that has since been re-registered), Record 31 (the arm-A side of the same blindness), Record 26 (an instrument that erased its own reading — the neighbouring failure family), registry **N263 / N268 / N269 / N270**, charter **Add.10 §A29.3 / §A30.1**.

Nothing above is deleted.** **(1) ✅ THE LEG EXISTS AND IT CAN FAIL:** `gate_addr_validated = true` as the AND of **nine** mechanical checks — ⭐ **this record's own arm (arm B's banked configuration) FAILS it 3/3**, a **planted permutation** scores **A1 = 0.0000 while `any_basin_rate` stays exactly 1.0000** (the defect class isolated: the two runs differ *only* in the declared target), a narrow-wells negative fails, and three scale controls hold. **(2) ⛔ AND THE DEFECT CLASS RECURRED SPONTANEOUSLY ON THE SPINE:** on **4 of 9** pass-3 cells **`any_basin` ≥ 0.98 while A1 ≤ 0.24** — ⭐ ***nearly every read lands in a basin; about one in four lands in the RIGHT one*** — and one cell (`simclr` seed 0) digs to the run's deepest median depth **0.942** with **15 of 16 capture radii exactly 0.0**, `any_basin = 0.0`, **A1 = 0.0**: ⛔ ***deep wells, no basins*** (**N282**). **(3) ⛔⛔ THE NEW AND WORSE VERSION OF THIS RECORD'S LESSON — the exam can ask the question now, and it rewards the wrong answer.** Across all nine cells **ρ(A1, drift) = −0.967** and **ρ(A1, settle↔launder agreement) = +0.933** ⇒ **the completed gate's score is a near-monotone function of settle-collapse: the more table-like the store, the higher it scores** — on the arm that works the settle agrees with nearest-key indexing **51.6 %** of the time (chance 0.0625) against **at chance** on the others. ⛔ **And the per-arm boolean built to catch exactly this read False on all three arms, decided by drift ties of 0.004 — a per-arm BOOLEAN is the wrong instrument for a DESIGN-LEVEL property** (**N278**). ⛔ **The gate is BARRED from further quotation until four named repairs land** (§A32.3). **(4) ⚖ AND THE VERDICT THIS RECORD'S RULING PRODUCED IS ITSELF RE-READ:** §A33.1 rules that **every leg is MECHANICS or VALUE**, that **VALUE legs exist only at TIER level with the tier's own control**, and that ⛔ **a launder margin on a COMPONENT gate is a DIAGNOSTIC, never a pass condition.** ⇒ ⭐ **the ratified filing form is *MECHANICS: all green; VALUE: untested by design at component level*** — ⚠ **which is not a promotion: the diagnostics are unchanged and still negative** (**N279**). ⭐ **G-ADDR is permanently re-scoped as a per-feature MECHANICS instrument, barred from VALUE duty** (§A34.8).
