## 11.27 Three pictures from C2W8 **PASS 3** and the wave's close: **the memory that answers and still loses**, **the strong encoder that was the worst address**, and **the exam that rewards collapse** **[plain-language records — new C2W8 pass 3, filed 2026-08-10]**

**Why this section exists — and the three sentences to read before the three records.** Pass 2 left the programme with repaired wells, an unmoved read, and a gate that could not fail on the difference. Pass 3 built the missing leg (**G-ADDR** — did the read land in **the queried item's** basin?), validated it on designed negatives **first**, put the whole completed gate on a **strong-φ** rig where the encoder was measurably not the bottleneck, and **pre-registered both outcomes as reportable before the first cell.** ⭐⭐ **Three things then happened at once: the pre-registered null fired exactly as registered; the wave's two pieces of supporting evidence for its own diagnosis were retracted by its own spokes; and the gate built to score it all was measured to reward the configuration the programme prohibits.** ⛔⛔ **Scope, binding on all three records: PASS 3 and the CLOSE only.** The **gate-hardening spoke has run nothing** (it gates the next wave) — ⛔ **its absence is a scope boundary, not a gap.** ⛔ **The arm A vs arm B race is UNADJUDICATED and no record below scores it.** ⛔ **Every reading is non-promotable; `γ_φ` is OFF; no claim cell ran; no tier-ii, full-CLU or I2 verdict exists; no paper number and no performance/ACC number exists; matching is matched-ITEMS everywhere.** ⭐⭐ **The wave verdict, in the ratified form (§A33.1): MECHANICS all green; VALUE untested by design at component level.** Registry **N274–N286**; ledger ⟲ **C2W8 pass-3 + close**; sources `c2w8p3-{gate-addr, phi-geometry, capture-strong-phi}` and charter **Add.11 §A31–§A32 / Add.12 §A33–§A34**, merged at **`main @ 9e0bb25`**, suite **1555/0** with HEAD verified unmoved across the run.

### Record 34 — **The memory that answers and still loses** (we finally measured where the read SETTLES instead of where it LAUNCHES — the store can address, and a copy of its own keys still beats it)

> **The memory that answers and still loses.** For two passes the programme believed it had a store whose queries never arrived. The evidence was a number that did not move: *58, 62, 62 reads out of 64 landing in no basin, digit-identical between passes.* ⛔⛔ **That number is computed where the read STARTS, not where it ENDS.** It asks whether the launch point is already close to a stored key — so with the same encoder and the same admitted items it returns **the same value no matter what the store does**. ⭐ ***A number that is identical across an intervention that should have moved it is usually a number that cannot see the intervention.*** **So the wave built the honest instrument** — did the read, launched from an item's own noisy address, settle into **that item's** basin? — **and proved it could fail before scoring anything with it:** the previous pass's gate-passing arm **fails** it on every seed; a control that keeps the same store and the same queries and only **mislabels which item was asked for** scores **zero**, while the old *"did it land in some basin?"* question scores **a perfect 1.0000 for both.** **And then the answer arrived, and it was the opposite of what everyone expected.** ⭐⭐ **Both arms address.** One reaches the correct well **about half the time**; the other **nine times in ten**. ⛔⛔ **And both still lose** — to the most trivial competitor imaginable: **a nearest-neighbour lookup over the very same keys the store holds, with no parameters, no physics and no training**, which scores **0.79–0.90** where the store scores **≈0.5**. ⇒ ⭐⭐ ***The store is not blind. It answers. It is simply beaten by a copy of its own address book.*** **And the explanation the programme had been carrying for that failure turned out to be arithmetic on the wrong population:** the "distance between neighbouring keys" it divided by was measured over a **200-key sizing sample** while the store holds **16 items**. Corrected, a query sits **three times closer to its own key than to its nearest neighbour** — the address space was *comfortable all along.* ⛔ **So the failure is real, it reproduces, and nobody can currently say why.**

**The same thing in numbers** (`c2w8p3-gate-addr` §4.1–§5, both arms re-scored **live** on 3 seeds; banked pass-2 legs reproduced to the digit first):

| what was measured | arm A (co-scaled width) | arm B (emission head) |
|---|---|---|
| ⛔ the **retired** launch-point counter (`n_unassigned` / 64) | **58 / 62 / 62** | **64 / 64 / 64** |
| ⭐ **A1 — read settles in the QUERIED item's basin** (threshold 0.25) | **0.5000 / 0.4219 / 0.5156** ✅ | **0.9297 / 0.9844 / 0.8750** ✅ |
| **A2 — items with zero correct cue reads** (settle-side; ≤ 0.5) | **0.2500 / 0.3750 / 0.2500** ✅ | 0.0625 / 0.0000 / 0.1250 ✅ |
| ⛔ **A3a — margin vs a kNN launder over the SAME keys** | **−0.3984 / −0.4375 / −0.2734** ❌ | −0.0156 / −0.0078 / **−0.0625** |
| ⛔ **A3b — margin on real held-out stream reads** | −0.3594 / −0.5000 / −0.2031 ❌ | ⛔ **−0.6094 / −0.6094 / −0.5625** ❌ |
| the launder's own score on the same cue | **0.8984** | — |
| ⇒ **G-ADDR** | ❌ **FAIL 3/3** | ❌ **FAIL 3/3** *(the designed negative)* |

**And the geometry that was supposed to explain the failure, measured on the right population:**

| population | σ_q / spacing | reading |
|---|---|---|
| the **~200-key sizing set** (what the retracted diagnosis quoted) | **≈ 1.07** | *"as far from its own key as from its neighbour"* — ⛔ **never-quote** |
| the **actual 16-item store**, MNIST | **0.316** | ⭐ **~3× closer to its own key** |
| the **16-item population**, CIFAR-10 | **0.19–0.37** | ⭐ same conclusion, second substrate |

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐ **The transferable statement, and it is the wave's sentence: *a memory can be genuinely addressable — the read arrives at the right well — and still add nothing, because a zero-parameter read of its own keys does the same job better.*** ⛔ **That is not a bug to fix; on this protocol it is a theorem showing up:** when the cue is a **noisy copy of a stored key**, nearest-neighbour over those keys **is the Bayes-optimal rule**, so losing to it is a known confirmation and not news. ⇒ ⭐ **the programme's own ruling: no further pass may be spent chasing daylight here** — the dividend, if it exists, can only live where the question is *not* metric-native: **compositional and unseen queries**, and **real streams.** ⭐⭐ **The second transferable lesson is methodological and cost two passes: state, for every telemetry number, WHERE IN THE TRAJECTORY it is evaluated** — the launch-side and settle-side versions of "did the read find its item?" differ here by **0.94 vs 0.25** on the very same cells. ⭐ **The third: register the POPULATION with every geometric ratio, exactly as this programme already registers the scale** — one population mismatch produced a retracted mechanism, a monitor whose 0.000 refusal rate was arithmetic, and a 30 % arm-dependent bias in the cue, and **one repair fixes all three.**

**Status: [⭐ measured, 3 seeds, both arms, `gate_addr_validated = true` as the AND of 9 mechanical checks with the designed negatives firing FIRST] · [⛔⛔ NEVER-QUOTE the launch-point statistic *"58/62/62, digit-identical"* — it is store-invariant BY CONSTRUCTION (N274)] · [⛔⛔ NEVER-QUOTE *"σ_q ≈ spacing ⇒ the items are not separable by any read"* — wrong population (N275); the failure is REAL, the CAUSE is OPEN and theorist-priced] · [⚠ the Hub's registered prior Q2 (A1 = 0.03–0.12) is FALSIFIED UPWARD to 0.42–0.52 and is recorded as falsified, not dropped] · [⚖ R3 DISCHARGED: `follow = 1.0079` at arm A's own kernel ⇒ the attractor CAN move off the stored key ⇒ capture was an OUTCOME, not algebraic pinning; the compliance ruling STANDS, no escalation — ⚠ with an honest CAPACITY limit: at ‖δ‖ = 0.60 the move saturates at 0.4116] · [⛔ the arm A vs arm B race is UNADJUDICATED — arm B appears here ONLY as a designed negative and no line ranks the two] · [⛔ matched-ITEMS everywhere; byte ratios 627× (arm A) / 665× (arm B) on the cue launder and pass 1's 1 253× on the stream launder, and ⛔ NO performance claim is made at any ratio] · [⛔ TOY, MNIST PCA `d = 8` census rig, non-promotable, `γ_φ` OFF, no claim cell, no tier-ii / full-CLU / I2 verdict] · [⛔ these numbers are NOT a baseline for any pass-3 CIFAR number (Head ruling R1)].** **Nomenclature (Def-2):** `σ_q` = **query jitter** (0.15, absolute), `κ_q` = the **dimensionless** cue scale (1.0), `spacing_ref` = the cell's own measured `median_nn_task1` (⚠ **the sizing-set quantity at the heart of erratum 2**), `ρ_i` = the item's **measured capture radius**, `follow = ‖q* − c‖/‖δ‖` — ⛔ **none of these is a mass; neither inertial $M$ nor spectral $\mu$ enters this record** (`kinetic_mode = newtonian_learned`, no mass axis varied); "depth" is a **fitted well depth**, ⛔ **never quotable as feature importance** (§A23.5 ACTIVE). **Reading pointers:** Record 31 **and its pass-3 update** (the record this one retracts and replaces), Record 33 + its pass-3 update (the gate that could not ask this question, and what happened when it could), Record 21 / **N213** (the settle-destroys-launch-information family the open mechanism question belongs to), Record 36 (why the completed gate's scores are themselves suspect), registry **N274 / N275 / N266**, charter **Add.11 §A31.1–§A31.2 / §A31.6**.

> **Update (C2W8 close-out, 2026-08-10) — THE TWO NEVER-QUOTES ARE NOW ENFORCED BY THE INSTRUMENT, NOT ONLY BY DOCTRINE; AND THE GEOMETRY REPAIR MOVED A KNOB NOBODY WAS WATCHING. Nothing above is deleted.** **(1) ✅ THE LAUNCH/SETTLE SPLIT IS IN CODE.** `_read_diagnostics` now emits **`launch_covered`** (documented alias, retained only because monitor `settle_argmin` needs it for Prop D1) **and `settle_covered`**, and the telemetry **gates on the settle side** — so `n_never_read` can no longer inherit the defect. ⭐⭐ **The designed negative that proves it is the cleanest demonstration in the wave: mutate the store so reads land elsewhere and `launch_covered` stays BIT-IDENTICAL at 8/8 → 8/8 (`np.array_equal` asserted) while `settle_covered` moves 8/8 → 0/8** (**N287**). **(2) ✅ THE POPULATION REPAIR LANDED — and it repairs CONSEQUENCES, not the mystery.** `d_safe` is now sized on the **store** population and the cue on the **codebook** spacing: store/sizing spacing **1.567 / 1.435 / 1.843**, refusal rate **0.000/0.250/0.000 → 0.250/0.250/0.143** (⚠ **direction predicted, magnitude explicitly not — reported, never tuned to a target**), `cue_σ/codebook_spacing` **1.000** against a legacy **1.566 / 6.014 / 2.116** (a **3.8×** swing across seeds). ⛔⛔ **The OPEN MECHANISM QUESTION is untouched: why the settle extracts less from the cue than 1-NN over the same keys, with comfortable geometry and real basins, is still unexplained and still theorist-priced.** ⚠ **And monitor #3 is STILL NOT FIXED — only its arithmetic cause is gone.** **(3) ⚠⚠ THE TRAP THIS UPDATE EXISTS TO WARN ABOUT:** every arm's store-config factory recovers the spacing as **`d_safe / d_safe_frac`**, so the repair **also moved each arm's co-scaled ATOM WIDTH** onto a population whose spacing is **~3× larger (0.445 vs 0.141)** ⇒ ⛔ **`atom_width_frac_spacing = 1.5` no longer means what it meant when it was selected; banked cells reproduce only with `d_safe_population = "sizing"`, which is for REPRODUCTION ONLY** (**N290**). Registry **N287 / N290**, charter **Add.11 §A32.3**, `PREREG-C2W11.md` §2.1.

### Record 35 — **The strong encoder that was the worst address** (we made the features better by the metric that defines "better", and the memory got worse at finding things)

> **The strong encoder that was the worst address.** The whole point of pass 3 was to remove an excuse. Every previous failure could be blamed on the address space: *of course nothing works, the features are PCA of raw pixels.* So the wave took the encoder it had **built, trained and priced** — a contrastive network, 8 000 steps, **+0.158 accuracy** over the PCA reference on the continual-learning benchmark, the best feature extractor the programme owns — and put the completed gate on it, with the weak PCA reference running **in the same run, at the same address dimension, on a bit-identical data stream** so that every comparison is paired. ⛔⛔ **The strong encoder came last.** On the leg that matters — does a read land in the right item's basin — it is worse than the **unfitted** control by **0.14** and worse than the **weak PCA reference** by **0.13**, beaten on **every seed of both comparisons**, and it is the arm whose items most often have **no basin at all** (15 of 16 on one seed). ⭐⭐ **And the control that beat it is free.** `randconv` is a randomly initialised convolutional trunk: it sees **no data**, takes **zero fit steps**, costs **2.9 seconds** against the strong encoder's **41 minutes per seed** — and it buys the *address geometry* the wave was trying to purchase, clearing the geometry rule at the strong arm's own margin. ⇒ ⭐⭐ ***What made the features good at the task made them bad at being addresses, and the part of "good features" that addressing actually needs was bought by the ARCHITECTURE, not by the objective.*** ⛔ **So *"strong φ"* was never one thing.** It was two properties that had never been measured apart, and when they were, **they moved in opposite directions.**

**The same thing in numbers** (`c2w8p3-capture-strong-phi` §3.1, `d = 12`, 3 seeds, streams bit-identical across arms ⇒ **paired**; `c2w8p3-phi-geometry` §2 for the geometry rows):

| arm | what it is | **CL accuracy** (banked) | **A1 — correct-basin** | items with **zero basin** / 16 | gate legs by seed |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **`simclr`** | contrastive, 8 000 steps, ≈41 min/seed | ⭐ **0.31912 ± 0.0050** | ⛔ **0.0964 ± 0.0482** | ⛔ **15 / 10 / 8** | **000 / 011 / 011** |
| **`randconv`** | **untrained conv trunk, 0 steps, 2.9 s** | 0.21296 ± 0.0063 | ⭐ **0.2370 ± 0.0052** | 5 / 3 / 7 | ⭐ **111 / 111 / 111** |
| **`pca`** | the weak reference, at the same `d` | 0.16080 ± 0.0038 | **0.2240 ± 0.0974** | 12 / 3 / **0** | 010 / 111 / 111 |

**Paired, on the same seed and the same stream** (negative = the strong arm is worse): **`simclr` − `randconv` = −0.1406 ± 0.0508, 0 of 3 seeds better** · **`simclr` − `pca` = −0.1276 ± 0.0589, 0 of 3** · `randconv` − `pca` = +0.0130 ± 0.1017 (**tied**). ⭐ **And the single best cell in the entire run belongs to the WEAK reference** (`pca` seed 2, A1 = **0.3828** — the only cell to clear all four legs).

**And the geometry, where the free control also ties or wins** (σ_q/spacing, lower = better, at n = 16): `pca` **0.370 / 0.334 / 0.313** · `simclr` **0.248 / 0.210 / 0.186** · **`randconv` 0.234 / 0.201 / 0.190** — the unfitted arm clears the same GO rule (**+0.1355 / +0.1334 / +0.1231**, 3/3 seeds) as the 8 000-step arm, and **at `d = 8` it is better** (`simclr − randconv` = **+0.0135 ± 0.0037**, positive = simclr worse, **0/3 seeds** simclr better).

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐ **The transferable rule: task accuracy and address quality are DIFFERENT PROPERTIES OF AN ENCODER, and on this substrate they are anti-correlated — so a memory should not be handed the representation that was optimised for the task.** ⭐ **This is the measured basis for three design decisions that were ratified the same week:** the **address block becomes its own head** (not a reuse of task features); **cheap, unfitted, conv-class geometry is a legitimate default** — it is *free*, and it is *better here*; and **the organizing objective is LABEL-FREE, with supervision entering only at the read head**, ⭐ *precisely because task supervision produced the worst address geometry we have measured.* ⚠⚠ **And the honest limits, both of which the measuring spoke published against itself.** **(1) The cue was not equally hard across the three arms** — normalised against a sizing set rather than the store's own codebook, it left a **30 % spread**, which **partially confounds *"randconv is best"*.** ⭐ **It does NOT rescue the strong arm: `simclr` had an EASIER cue than `pca` and still scored below it.** **(2) We know THAT the fitted encoder addresses worse; we do not know WHY** — two candidates are live and cheap (over-overlapping compact supports at the strong arm's tighter key spacing; or the l2-normalised contrastive features concentrating the addresses **on a shell**), and ⛔ **one width sweep at fixed φ would decide it, and was not run.**

**Status: [⭐ measured, 9 census cells, 3 arms × 3 seeds, PAIRED on bit-identical streams (fingerprints verified), with the weak-φ reference re-run INTERNALLY at the same `d` — ⛔ no pass-1/2 census number is a baseline here (Head ruling R1)] · [⭐ encoder identity verified bit-identical to the banked price (`loss_first = 5.512242317199707`, 225 536 φ floats) ⇒ the SAME built-and-priced encoder, not a refit] · [⛔⛔ "strong φ" may NEVER again be used as one undifferentiated notion; the admissible form names WHICH strength, and the CIFAR artifact's mandatory provenance sentence still travels BESIDE this qualifier, never replaced by it] · [⛔ Add.10 §A29.5's "φ is the binding constraint on the CL rig" does NOT survive] · [⚠ CONFOUND, declared: cue difficulty varies 30 % across arms (0.927 / 0.875 / 0.710) — it qualifies every cross-arm number and does NOT rescue the strong arm] · [⚠ the mechanism of the inversion is NOT established — measured, unattributed, one cheap cell would decide it] · [⚠ `randconv` misses the A1 threshold by ONE READ on 2/3 seeds (31/31/29 of 128 against 32/128), and a LEGAL rescale inside the registered guard flips that leg's verdict — a seed-count question, declared NOT-RUN] · [⛔ one substrate, one `d`, `task1_only` only; `generic_frozen` and `convae` NOT RUN; the pass-3 rig scores NO accuracy number at all] · [⛔ non-promotable, no claim cell, no tier-ii / full-CLU / I2 verdict].** **Nomenclature (Def-2):** $\varphi$ is a frozen **read-in map** (an address space), ⛔ **never a model of the data**; the **map** is `PhiProjection(form="pca")`, `φ_dim = 256 → addr_dim = 12`, fitted `task1_only` and frozen, with its **3 328 floats on EVERY arm's ledger including the launder**; `s` = **atom width** (co-scaled, achieved 0.621–0.856); ⛔ **neither inertial $M$ nor spectral $\mu$ enters this record.** **Reading pointers:** Record 30 **and its pass-3 update** (the accuracy ladder this inverts — the same encoder, the other axis), Record 34 (the leg these arms are scored on), Record 36 (why the arm that "works" here is also the most table-like one — read the two together or neither), registry **N277 / N281 / N280**, charter **Add.11 §A31.4 · Add.12 §A34.6 / §A34.10**.

### Record 36 — **The exam that rewards collapse** (the gate we built to detect a memory scored it highest exactly when it stopped being one — and the flag we built to catch that was decided by a rounding error)

> **The exam that rewards collapse.** This programme has one prohibited outcome, written down long before this wave: a store whose settled answer is a **deterministic function of the stored key**. That is a lookup table wearing a physics costume — it can be replaced by a dictionary, and everything the physics is for has vanished. The pass-2 gate's prettiest number was exactly the symptom of it (the settled point sitting a hundredfold closer to the recorded site than the keys are to each other), and the Advisor filed a standing warning: ⛔ **never treat that number as a target.** ⭐⭐ **Pass 3 obeyed the warning — no leg, objective or tuning choice targeted it — and then measured something worse: across all nine cells, the COMPLETED GATE'S SCORE IS VERY NEARLY A MONOTONE FUNCTION OF HOW COMPLETELY THE PHYSICS COLLAPSED.** Rank-correlate the gate's addressability score against the collapse measure and you get **−0.967**; against how often the settle agrees with the trivial lookup, **+0.933**. **The cell that scored best in the whole run is the cell whose settle agrees with the lookup most often and whose drift is essentially the lowest.** ⇒ ⭐⭐ ***The exam was handing out its highest marks for being a table.*** **And the safeguard failed in the most ordinary way imaginable.** The wave had a mechanical flag for precisely this — *is the best-scoring arm also the lowest-drift arm?* — and it reported **"no" on all three arms.** ⛔ **It was decided by differences of 0.004.** A per-arm yes/no question, evaluated three times, **reported the exact opposite of a relationship that is nearly perfect when you look across all nine cells at once.** ⭐ ***A boolean is the wrong instrument for a property of the design; the correlation was there the whole time, and only the per-arm view hid it.***

**The same thing in numbers** (`c2w8p3-capture-strong-phi` §5; n = 9 cells, all three arms × three seeds):

| statistic | value | what it means |
|---|---|---|
| **Spearman ρ(A1, drift ratio)** | ⛔⛔ **−0.9667** | the less the settle moves, the higher the gate scores |
| **Spearman ρ(A1, settle↔launder agreement)** | ⛔⛔ **+0.9333** | the more the settle imitates the lookup, the higher the gate scores |
| Pearson equivalents | −0.8774 / +0.9072 | not a rank artefact |
| the mechanical flag `best_is_also_lowest_drift` | **False on 3/3 arms** — decided by **0.004** ties | ⛔ **the safeguard reported "clear"** |

**And the mechanism, visible per-arm** — *the working arm is the most table-like arm:*

| arm | settle agrees with nearest-key indexing (chance **0.0625**) | ‖settle − launder's key‖ ÷ spacing | gate outcome |
|---|---|---|---|
| **`randconv`** (the arm that addresses best) | ⛔ **0.516 / 0.484 / 0.266** | **0.44× spacing** (seed 0) | best A1 |
| `simclr` / `pca` (the arms that address worst) | **at chance** (0.055–0.070 on the worst cells) | **~2× spacing** | worst A1 |

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐ **The doctrine, now standing: *register, with every gate leg, the degenerate limit that would MAXIMISE it — and then check whether your gate's total score is monotone in that limit, across the whole design rather than per-arm.*** This is the mirror of the wave's earlier lesson. Pass 2 taught *a gate must be able to fail on the thing that matters*; pass 3 adds ⭐ ***a gate must not be able to PASS by becoming the thing you forbid.*** ⛔ **The consequence was applied immediately and it is expensive: no future census number is quotable until four named repairs land** — a two-sided drift leg or a drift **floor**; the addressability leg reported as a **margin in standard errors** rather than against a discrete threshold the best arm missed **by one read**; a ruling that the only legal rescale is a **full-state** one (the wave found a *legal* rescale that kept its metric inside the registered guard and still **flipped a leg's verdict**); and a fix to the launch-point counter of Record 34. ⭐ **And a second, quieter correction rides with it:** the addressability instrument is **permanently re-scoped as a MECHANICS instrument** — it may say *"the mechanism works"* and it is **barred from ever being asked whether the mechanism is worth anything.** ⭐⭐ **That last distinction is the wave's most portable product: every gate leg is either MECHANICS (does it work) or VALUE (is it worth anything), VALUE can only be measured at system level against a system-level control, and a component gate that tries to answer a VALUE question will report a failure it was never entitled to test** — which is exactly what pass 3's launder leg did, and why *"the completed gate failed on all three arms"* is **not** this wave's result.

**Status: [⭐ measured across 9 cells; the D2a probe was run TWO-SIDED and no leg, objective or tuning choice treated drift → 0 as a target] · [⛔⛔ the completed gate is BARRED FROM FURTHER QUOTATION until the four repairs land (§A32.3) — mine the DEFECTS, not the gate scores] · [⛔ this is a property of the GATE'S DESIGN, not a verdict on any arm — and it does NOT license reading a LOW gate score as "healthy physics" either] · [⭐⭐ the ratified wave verdict is MECHANICS all green / VALUE untested by design at component level; the earlier "failed on all three arms" reading is SUPERSEDED, struck and dated, not deleted — ⚠ and this is NOT a promotion, because the diagnostics are unchanged and still negative] · [⚖ compliance is NOT in question: R3 discharged at `follow = 1.0079`, so the near-zero drift is an OUTCOME of the write objective and capture was earned, not pinned] · [⚠ the gate-hardening spoke that owns the repairs HAS RUN NOTHING at filing time — its absence here is scope, not a gap] · [⛔ TOY, 9 cells, one substrate, one `d`, non-promotable, no claim cell, no VALUE leg anywhere in this wave].** **Nomenclature (Def-2):** **G-DRIFT** = median site drift ÷ the measured key spacing (a **dimensionless ratio**, reported two-sided); **D2a** = the *table-expressible* configuration (settled point ≈ a deterministic function of the stored key) prohibited by intervention §8.2; **MECHANICS vs VALUE** per §A33.1; ⛔ **no mass of either kind enters this record**, and "depth" is a **fitted well depth**, ⛔ **never feature importance** (§A23.5 ACTIVE). **Reading pointers:** Record 33 **and its pass-3 update** (the same defect class from the other side — a gate that cannot fail vs a gate that can pass wrongly), Record 31's pass-2 warning (which named this risk before it was measured), Record 15 (*a memory that works perfectly is a memory a table can copy* — the same tension, three waves earlier), Record 34 (the leg whose scores this record fences), registry **N278 / N279 / N280 / N282**, charter **Add.11 §A31.5–§A31.6 / §A32.3 · Add.12 §A33.1 / §A34.8**.

> **Update (C2W8 close-out, 2026-08-10) — THE EXAM IS REPAIRED, AND THE PROOF IS THAT A PLANTED TABLE NOW FAILS WHERE IT USED TO PASS. Nothing above is deleted.** **(1) ✅ THE REPAIR, MEASURED ON A PLANTED CONSTRUCTION RATHER THAN ASSERTED.** The drift leg is now **two-sided** with a **FLOOR of `0.01 × the MEASURED codebook spacing`** (⛔ never a bare constant; derivation registered **before** the code, and sitting **2×** above a banked table-expressible point at ~0.005× spacing), and `fails_high` is named apart from **`fails_low_D2a_table_expressible`**. ⛔⛔ **A store planted to be table-like — atoms exactly at their own sites, 4 items on a ring — reads drift/spacing 0.0042 and now FAILS, while `one_sided_pass2_pass = True`: the retired rule passes exactly the configuration the programme forbids** (**N287**). **(2) ✅ THE OTHER THREE REPAIRS.** A1 now ships **`margin_in_se_vs_threshold`, `n_correct`, `n_correct_needed`, `reads_to_flip`** beside every boolean; the scale guard's **pass condition is VERDICT STABILITY** (the metric bound demoted to a diagnostic) with **`LEGAL_RESCALE` = full-state co-scaling incl. payload** — ⭐ **and fed this record's own banked pair (A1 0.24219 → 0.28906) the repaired guard returns `metric_bounded = True` AND `verdict_stable = False` ⇒ FAIL**; the census **REFUSES a non-selected atom width, loudly**; and `P` is never emitted without `P_comparability`. **(3) ⭐ THE BAR IS DISCHARGED — AND THE NEW BAR IS HIGHER.** `gate_hardening_done = true`, **12/12 items**, `main @ 2e1cdb2`, suite **1570/0** ⇒ **census numbers are quotable again, with the instrument that produced them NAMED.** ⚠⚠ **The repaired census is STRICTLY HARDER: a real banked pass-3 cell (arm A, seed 0) sits at 0.0071 and would FAIL the repaired leg** ⇒ **a future cell failing there is not a regression in the physics** (**N291**; ⚖ the Hub is asked to confirm the floor first). **(4) ⛔ AND THE VERDICT-SIDE CONSEQUENCE, which is a rule applied in code and NOT a result:** `gate_addr_pass = A1 AND A2` with A3 `in_pass_condition: False` ⇒ **arm B's banked configuration, which failed only on A3, passes both MECHANICS legs** (settle-side A1 = 0.9297/0.9844/0.8750) — ⛔ **no cell was re-run, the race is VOID, and arm B stays claim-barred by `NO_TIER_II_CLAIM`** (**N288**). ## 11.28 One picture from the C2W8 **CLOSE-OUT**: **the test that was made out of the thing it was testing** **[plain-language record — new at the C2W8 close, filed 2026-08-10]**

**Why this section exists.** The three records above (34–36) are about what the wave *measured*. This one is about what the wave found when it went back to repair the *instruments* that produced those measurements — and it is the only C2W8 record whose subject is a **test** rather than a store. ⛔⛔ **Scope: the close-out spoke only. NO banked arm was re-run; nothing here re-scores any number; no C2W11 cell has run.** ⛔ **The arm A vs arm B race stays VOID and UNADJUDICATED; no tier-ii, full-CLU or I2 verdict, no paper number, no performance/ACC number exists.** Position **`main @ 2e1cdb2`**, suite **1570 passed / 0 failed**, HEAD verified identical before and after. Registry **N287–N293**; ledger ⟲ **C2W8 CLOSE-FINAL**; source `c2w8-close-gate-hardening` + the **`[C2W8-CLOSE-FINAL]`** §10 entry.

### Record 37 — **The test that was made out of the thing it was testing** (we certified that our exam could fail by feeding it a number we had just retired — and the repair proved itself by failing one of our own results)

> **The test that was made out of the thing it was testing.** An instrument is only trustworthy if you can show it says **no** to something. So when this programme built its addressability exam, it attached a *designed negative*: a configuration that **must fail**, asserted in the test suite, so that a passing grade means something. The chosen configuration was a store arm from the previous pass, and the failure was demonstrated by feeding the exam that arm's **recorded telemetry**. ⛔⛔ **The number fed in was the very statistic the wave had just retired as meaningless** — a count evaluated where the read *starts* rather than where it *ends*, which is the same whatever the store does. ⇒ ⭐⭐ ***The certificate that the exam could fail had been manufactured out of the defect the exam was being repaired for.*** **The fix was not to delete the test.** Deleting it would have removed the record of falsifiability along with the flaw. It was **rewritten**, with the truth in its docstring, and the exam's falsifiability now rests entirely on **planted** constructions — stores built on purpose to be wrong, run through the shipped code. ⭐⭐ **And the strongest of those planted tests is worth stating on its own, because it is what "the bar moved" means in this programme:** build a store that is deliberately a lookup table — every atom sitting exactly on its own item's site — and the **old** rule gives it a pass, while the **repaired** rule fails it. ⭐ **Then the repair did the thing that makes a repair credible: it failed one of our own banked results.** A cell from the previous pass sits just below the new floor, so the census we now ship is **strictly harder than the one every number in this wave was measured on.** ⇒ **that is recorded, and nothing is re-scored.** **And one repair moved something nobody was watching.** The quantity being fixed was recovered *by division* inside every arm's configuration factory — so correcting which population it was measured over silently rescaled each arm's **atom width** by about three. ⛔ ***A setting whose value had been chosen by measurement no longer means what it meant when it was chosen***, and the only honest response is to re-select it and say so.

**The same thing in numbers** (`c2w8-close-gate-hardening` §1–§3; **12/12 items**, both mandatory designed negatives **measured**, all five pre-registered predictions held, none tuned):

| the designed negative | what the RETIRED instrument said | what the REPAIRED instrument says |
|---|---|---|
| a **planted table-like store** (atoms on their own sites; depth 4.0, width 0.12, radius 0.9, 4 items on a ring) — drift/spacing **0.0042** against a floor of **0.0100** | ⛔ **PASS** (`one_sided_pass2_pass = True`) | ⭐ **FAIL**, and it says WHY: `fails_low_D2a_table_expressible = True`, `fails_high = False` |
| a **mutated store** (learned atom amplitudes zeroed, so reads land elsewhere) | `launch_covered` **8/8 → 8/8, bit-identical** (`np.array_equal` asserted) — ⛔ **the statistic cannot see the change** | `settle_covered` **8/8 → 0/8** — ⭐ **the settle-side statistic moves** |
| the banked **A1 pair 0.24219 → 0.28906** under a *legal* rescale | ⛔ **PASS** — Δ = 0.04688 ≤ the registered 0.05 metric bound | ⭐ **FAIL** — `metric_bounded = True` **and** `verdict_stable = False` (the 0.25 threshold is straddled) |

**And the repair's own side-effects, both reported by the spoke against its own work:**

| quantity | before (sizing population) | after (store population) |
|---|---|---|
| store-spacing ÷ sizing-spacing | — | **1.567 / 1.435 / 1.843** |
| `d_safe` | 0.0276 / 0.0407 / 0.0264 | **0.0433 / 0.0584 / 0.0486** |
| monitor-#3 refusal rate (⚠ **reported, never tuned to a target**) | 0.000 / 0.250 / 0.000 | **0.250 / 0.250 / 0.143** |
| `cue_σ` ÷ spacing (the cross-arm fairness quantity) | **1.566 / 6.014 / 2.116** — a **3.8×** swing across seeds | **1.000 / 1.000 / 1.000** |
| an arm's co-scaled **atom width** | chosen against spacing **0.141** | ⛔ **now co-scales against 0.445 — the SAME flag, a ~3× different store** |

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐⭐ **The doctrine, and it is the most portable thing in this section: *a designed negative must be a planted construction, never a banked number.*** A negative built from recorded telemetry can only be as trustworthy as that telemetry — which is **exactly what is in question** when you are repairing an instrument. ⭐ **The corollary that saved the record here: rewrite the negative, never delete it** — deletion removes the audit trail along with the flaw. ⭐⭐ **The second lesson is about repairs in general: when a quantity is recovered by DIVISION inside other components (`d_safe / d_safe_frac`), fixing it moves every consumer, invisibly.** The right response is what the spoke did — **declare the propagation in code, name the reproduction switch, and say which banked cells stop reproducing** — and the wrong one is to assume that a fix in one place is a fix in one place. ⭐ **The third is a standard this programme can now hold itself to: a repaired instrument should be able to fail your own past results, and if it cannot, ask whether it was really repaired.** Ours fails a banked cell of ours at 0.0071 against a 0.0100 floor, and that is the evidence that the bar actually moved rather than being re-described.

**Status: [⭐ instrument repair, `gate_hardening_done = true` as the AND of 12 mechanical items, both mandatory designed negatives MEASURED on planted rigs, 5/5 pre-registered predictions held with none tuned] · [⛔⛔ NO banked arm was re-run — every consequence below is a MECHANICAL consequence of a ratified rule or a repaired instrument, ⛔ never a re-score and ⛔ never an arm-race adjudication (the race is VOID, §A30.1)] · [⛔ arm B's post-repair pass on the two MECHANICS legs is a consequence of §A33.1 applied in code, NOT evidence that the arm works; arm B stays claim-barred by `NO_TIER_II_CLAIM` (N288)] · [⚠ the repaired census is STRICTLY HARDER — a banked pass-3 cell would now fail the drift leg; ⚖ the Hub is asked to confirm the floor before C2W11 scores anything (N291)] · [⚠ banked co-scaled-width cells reproduce ONLY with `d_safe_population = "sizing"`, which is for REPRODUCTION ONLY and never a claim cell (N290)] · [⛔ the OPEN MECHANISM QUESTION is untouched — the repair fixes three CONSEQUENCES of the population defect and explains nothing about the settle] · [⚠ monitor #3 is STILL NOT FIXED; only its arithmetic cause is removed] · [⛔ the regression cells are a TOY synthetic rig (`addr_dim = 3`, capacity 6, quick mode) and are explicitly NOT science cells] · [⛔ TOY, non-promotable, no claim cell, no tier-ii / full-CLU / I2 verdict, no paper number, no performance/ACC number].** **Nomenclature (Def-2):** **G-DRIFT** = median site drift ÷ the **measured codebook spacing** (a dimensionless ratio, now two-sided with a floor); `d_safe` = the **admission** separation threshold, now sized on the **store** population; `κ_q` / `cue_σ` = the **dimensionless** cue scale, now normalised on **codebook** spacing; **`launch_covered` vs `settle_covered`** = the same question asked at the **start** and at the **end** of the trajectory, and ⛔ **they are different quantities that may never be substituted**; **MECHANICS vs VALUE** per §A33.1; ⛔ **no mass of either kind enters this record — neither inertial $M$ nor spectral $\mu$** — and "depth" is a **fitted well depth**, ⛔ **never feature importance** (§A23.5 ACTIVE). **Reading pointers:** Record 34 (the launch-vs-settle distinction this record ships in code), Record 36 (the exam this record repairs — read them as a pair), Record 33 (*a gate that cannot fail*), registry **N287 / N288 / N289 / N290 / N291 / N292 / N293** and the standing **RETIRED INSTRUMENTS AND RE-LABELLINGS** block, charter **Add.11 §A31.5–§A31.6 / §A32.3 · Add.12 §A33.1**, and `PREREG-C2W11.md` §2.1 for what the repairs bind forward.

## 11.29 Two pictures from **C2W10**: **the venue that a box of old examples already wins**, and **the ruler that measured its own rounding error** **[plain-language records — new at the C2W10 close, filed 2026-08-11]**

**Why this section exists.** C2W10 set out to ask whether a **persistent memory** — one that survives the boundary between streams and keeps regimes it saw before — is worth anything on a real, drifting data stream. It never got to ask. **Both candidate venues were measured, before anything was built on them, and on both of them a plain box of remembered examples at the same number of stored bytes already sat at or above the strong-baseline frontier.** The wave closed as a **MECHANICS** wave: the machinery ships and is quotable; ⛔⛔ **the VALUE surface is a DECLARED NOT-RUN for want of an admissible venue — never a null, in any artifact, ever.** ⛔ **No CLU cell of any kind ran in either gate.** ⭐⭐ **And the framing is ruled, not chosen: this is a FALLBACK being correctly retired, not a venue crisis** — the primary track (bounded-state sequence modelling) is live on CSF3 and the compositional family is live in C2W11. Position `main @ a47ed20`, suite **1679 passed / 0 failed**, HEAD verified identical before and after, zero worktrees. Registry **N294–N303**; ledger ⟲ **C2W10**; sources `c2w10-{benchmark-scout, benchmark-gate, metro-gate, lifecycle-mechanics, i2-usage-erosion}` + the **`[C2W10-CLOSE]`** §10 entry + charter **Add.14 §A39–§A41**.

### Record 38 — **The venue that a box of old examples already wins** (we tested both of our benchmarks before building on them; a bounded exemplar store beat us to both, and on one of them shuffling the data helped it)

> **The venue that a box of old examples already wins.** Before spending a wave building a persistent memory on a benchmark, this programme runs a **tripwire**: give a *dumb* competitor exactly the same number of stored bytes we would spend, and see whether it already sits at the frontier. The dumb competitor is a **bounded exemplar store** — literally a box that keeps the last few thousand examples and answers new questions by finding the nearest ones. ⭐ **The tripwire ran FIRST, at roughly a fiftieth of the cost of the build it was protecting** — the whole point being that discovering a venue is inadmissible *after* the build is the expensive ordering, and it is the one that is free to avoid. **On the primary venue it fired by a whisker; on the fallback it fired by a mile.** On the first — an insect-classification stream whose hidden regime variable is a temperature the model never sees — the byte-matched box scored **76.92 %** against a strong ensemble's **78.81 %**, and a box **five times smaller** landed within **0.07 points** of the published state of the art. ⛔ **And the mechanism is the uncomfortable part: on that stream, storing MORE makes you WORSE** — the store sized to our own byte budget was the **worst** arm of all, nineteen points below one thirty times smaller. *Recency was doing the job the hidden regime variable was supposed to require memory for.* On the second — six years of hourly motorway traffic, forecast a day ahead with the clock hidden — the box didn't merely tie: it **beat a tuned gradient-boosted ensemble that carries five times its bytes**, and it beat **every one of nine** strong references. ⭐⭐ **Then the third measurement made the venue impossible rather than merely unfavourable: shuffle the stream into random order, destroying every trace of time, and the byte-matched box gets BETTER.** ⇒ ***at the budgets we would have used, that stream's ordering carries nothing for a memory to be good at.*** **Two honest details make the result trustworthy rather than convenient.** First, the analyst gave the *competitor* every advantage the rules require: it ran with and without feature scaling and we consumed the better — ⛔ **and that choice is what fired the first tripwire; had it run the crippled version, the gate would have said "go".** Second, on the second venue the analyst found a leak **in its own pre-registered protocol** — a one-day-ahead forecast scored the ordinary way hands a continuously-updating learner up to **23 hours of future traffic** — and the leak was **biased in favour of firing its own tripwire.** ⭐ *It closed the leak anyway, reported both protocols, and the verdict survived.*

**The same thing in numbers** (`c2w10-benchmark-gate` §3/§5, `c2w10-metro-gate` §3/§6 + their gate JSONs; all at `m = 1`, repo HEAD `7fcef50` unchanged, scratch venvs, project lock untouched):

| venue | byte-matched exemplar store | best strong reference | margin | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **INSECTS** `incremental-reoccurring` | SAM-kNN `L=5000` @ **0.634 MiB** = **76.9157 %** | ARF-100 **78.8139 ± 0.0526** (state **9,542,925 B = 14.35×**) | **−1.8983 pts** | ⛔ **fires** — and against *all five* ARF references (−1.94 … **−0.2143** vs the published 77.13) |
| **Metro Interstate** (hidden clock, 24 h) | k-NN over past windows @ **0.634 MiB** = **MAE 314.575** | `gbdt_tuned` **335.203** (state **3,618,071 B = 5.44×**) | **−6.15 % rel.** | ⛔ **fires against all nine** strong references (−0.062 … −0.297) |

| the two byte-frontiers, and they point opposite ways | 250 | 500 | 1,000 | 2,000 | 5,000 | **14,782 / 14,894 (our own budget)** |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **INSECTS** accuracy % (regime variable **withheld** from the query) | 75.56 | **76.03** | 75.36 | 73.39 | 68.15 | ⛔ **59.75 — the worst arm** |
| **Metro** MAE (the clock is **inside** each query's own 24-lag window) | 407.91 | 347.89 | 327.79 | 325.22 | 320.98 | ⭐ **306.76 — still improving** |

| the two checks that made Metro *impossible*, not merely unfavourable | measured |
|---|---|
| headroom of the best tuned ensemble over a **one-line weekly rule** `ŷ = y(t−168 h)` | ⛔ **2.17 %** (335.203 vs 342.651) |
| destroying the stream order (fixed-seed pair shuffle) at **0.634 MiB** | ⛔ **320.982 → 311.748, −2.9 % — it HELPS** (and −1.9 % at our own budget; it only *hurts*, +4.71 %, **below** ~2,000 exemplars) |
| the same shuffle's positive control (persistence must be invariant) | ✅ **574.1346849611 both ways, diff exactly 0** |
| the 24-h label leak the analyst found in its own prereg | **+10.85 %** to a 250-exemplar k-NN · **−0.34 %** to GBDT ⇒ **asymmetric in the direction of firing** |

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐⭐ **The transferable rule is about venue selection and it now has six confirmations behind it: *if the answer to a query is a function of the query itself, a table of remembered examples is the Bayes-optimal machine and physics cannot be paid for* — so a memory claim must be shopped where that is provably not true.** ⛔ **The programme's response is to believe its own theorem rather than buy a seventh confirmation: no third tabular stream is shopped, and the absence of one is a RULING, not an omission.** ⭐⭐ **Three cheap screens came out of this wave and any of them kills a bad venue in under an hour:** measure headroom against **the strongest trivial rule, not against persistence** (on Metro those read 41.6 % and **2.17 %** — both true, only the second informative); check that the **regime variable is OUTSIDE the query**; and **shuffle the stream** — ⛔ ***a venue where destroying the order does not hurt a byte-matched store cannot host a retention claim, whatever the tripwire says.*** ⭐ **And the meta-lesson about ordering, which cost this wave almost nothing and would otherwise have cost it everything: test the venue BEFORE you build on it, and let the competitor be as strong as the rules allow — the anti-hobbling rule is the reason the first gate fired at all.**

**Status: [⭐ two admissibility instruments, both pre-registered, both with their own positive controls (loader B1 reproduces the published persistence baseline to 0.0074 points; the shuffle leaves persistence bit-identical); ~5 h of CPU total] · [⛔⛔ NO CLU cell of any kind ran in either gate — these are statements about BENCHMARKS, never about the store] · [⛔⛔ the VALUE surface is a DECLARED NOT-RUN for want of an admissible venue, ⛔ NEVER a null] · [⚠ INSECTS fired by 0.10 points inside its threshold against our strongest reference — the honest sentence is *"the byte-matched exemplar store is at ARF's shoulder"*, not *"it beats it"*; Metro fired by 4–25 % and also on a static holdout] · [⚠ Metro's feature vector, gap-fill and drift annotation are OURS, single-seed on the strong baselines, and its regime map is not the literature's — every artifact says so] · [⛔ `out-of-control`, the published drift-free null, HAS NO DATA SOURCE; Metro's shuffle is a RE-CONSTRUCTION with four declared limits] · [⛔ INSECTS band `b = 4` is contaminated (No-Change 91 %, every arm 97–99 %) ⇒ `R(4)`/`A(4)` uninterpretable] · [⛔ *"ARF is the reference"* never travels without its byte caveat — ARF is byte-matched to nothing].** **Nomenclature (Def-2):** *state bytes* = the arm's own stored state, measured, never estimated; *exemplar store* = a bounded raw-example memory queried in the input metric (SAM-kNN / windowed k-NN), which is this programme's standing **laundering control**, not a rival architecture; ⛔ **no mass of either kind enters this record — neither inertial $M$ nor spectral $\mu$** — and *depth* does not appear at all. **Reading pointers:** Record 30 (*the better the address, the less the physics adds* — the same theorem measured from inside the store), Record 8 (the launder that deletes the physics), Record 14 (when a benchmark grades your construction back at you), registry **N294 / N295 / N296 / N300**, charter **Add.14 §A39.1–§A39.2 / §A41.1 / §A41.3**.

### Record 39 — **The ruler that measured its own rounding error** (a proxy can be highly repeatable and still be nothing at all — reliability is necessary, not sufficient)

> **The ruler that measured its own rounding error.** This programme learned, one wave earlier, that a measurement whose repeatability is zero cannot support any conclusion: *if two halves of the same data disagree completely, no correlation you compute from it means anything.* So the natural next move is to check repeatability first — and this wave did, on the quantity meant to capture **undesigned erosion**: how much a stored memory's depth decays *beyond* the decay the design deliberately applies. The check came back **excellent**: split the run in half and the two halves agree at **0.78–0.89**. The analyst had pre-registered the opposite (*"it is float noise, so it should not reproduce"*) and was **refuted.** ⭐⭐ **Then it asked the second question, which nobody had written down as a rule: how BIG is the thing being measured?** After netting out the designed decay, the total drift over a whole run was **four hundred nanonats** — against **0.6 nats** of designed decay in the same run, a ratio of **seven ten-millionths**, and **about four units in the last place of a 32-bit float.** More than half the memories showed netted *increases* as well as decreases. ⇒ ⛔ ***the proxy was measuring round-off — and the round-off is deterministic and item-specific, which is exactly why it split-halves beautifully.*** **A stable ruler pointed at nothing is still a stable ruler.** ⭐ **The correction is not to distrust reliability but to pair it:** a proxy needs *(a)* repeatability **and** *(b)* a magnitude comparable to the channel it claims to see. And the practical consequence is blunt: **at 32-bit precision the instrument's noise floor sits above any effect it could possibly detect**, so any future measurement of undesigned erosion has to store its amplitudes in **64-bit** or it is measuring nothing, however carefully.

**The same thing in numbers** (`c2w10-i2-usage-erosion` §3.3/§3.6 + its PREREG scorecard; 3 seeds, `addr_dim = 12`, 48 chunks, float32 store amplitudes):

| quantity | seed 0 | seed 1 | seed 2 |
|---|---|---|---|
| split-half reliability of `E_netted` (**pre-registered ≤ 0.20 — REFUTED**) | **0.885** | **0.839** | **0.778** |
| median **netted** total log-drop over the whole run | **4.81e-07 nats** | **3.98e-07** | **4.49e-07** |
| median **raw** total log-drop (the designed channel) | 0.580 nats | 0.590 | 0.620 |
| ratio netted ÷ raw | ⛔ **6.95e-7** | ⛔ **6.77e-7** | ⛔ **6.77e-7** |
| netted span in float32 **ULP** (median / max) | 4.12 / 6.79 | 3.65 / 6.74 | 3.77 / 6.53 |
| wells whose netted curve *rises* as well as falls | 54 / 57 | 58 / 60 | 57 / 57 |

**The proposed rule, in the form it was filed** (⛔ **PROPOSED, Advisor-facing — not adopted by the Hub and not by this document**):

> **Reliability is necessary but not sufficient.** Every erosion/usage proxy carries **two** checks: **(a) reliability** (ICC / split-half) **and (b) magnitude against the designed channel.** **A proxy at ≤ 1e-6 of the designed channel is UNDEFINED regardless of its reliability.**

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐ **The pair of rules now reads as one instrument doctrine, and each half catches a different way of being fooled.** *C2W6:* **`ICC ≤ 0 ⇒ UNDEFINED`** — an unreliable proxy has an attenuation ceiling of zero, so no true effect of any size could have shown up. *C2W10:* **reliable ≠ valid** — a perfectly repeatable proxy can be a repeatable measurement of arithmetic. ⭐ ***Repeatability tells you the instrument is stable; it never tells you the instrument is pointed at anything.*** ⛔ **The concrete consequences travel with the rule:** any future undesigned-erosion cell runs store amplitudes in **float64**; the detection floor for this wave's correlations is **0.272 / 0.265 / 0.272**, not the registered *"≈ 0.25"* (because `n_live = 64` was **transient** and the scored populations were 57 / 60 / 57); and a decision threshold must be chosen **jointly with `n`** — here, clearing the registered bar at the registered `n` would have needed a correlation of **−0.47**, which the same document called sufficient power. ⭐ **The generalisable habit that produced all of this: pre-register the boring prediction too.** *"This will be noise"* was pre-registered, refuted, and the refutation is the finding — an un-pre-registered agreement would have been neither.

**Status: [⭐ instrument finding, 3 seeds, measured on a MECHANICS rig; the rule itself is PROPOSED and ⛔ NOT adopted — the Advisor owns it] · [⛔ this is a statement about a PROXY, not about the store: it neither confirms nor refutes that undesigned erosion exists] · [⛔ the companion channel finding is separate and independent — at this rig there is NO outer objective and NO optimizer step on store parameters, so the hypothesised mechanism could not act at all (a DECLARED NOT-RUN, never a null)] · [⛔ synthetic substrate = MECHANICS instrument, NEVER a claim venue] · [⛔ depth is not feature importance (§A23.5 ACTIVE and TRAVELLING); ⛔ never quote `ρ(U, depth_raw) = +0.18/+0.22/+0.19` as positive evidence — it is below the detection floor and age-confounded; ⛔ never quote the pooled-across-seeds estimator, which FLIPS SIGN] · [⚠ netting itself is what exposed this: it is a BUILD REQUIREMENT, every curve raw AND netted, and the registered 1e-9 analytic tolerance holds only in float64 — the shipped floor is ≈5.3e-8].** **Nomenclature (Def-2):** `E` = an **erosion rate** fitted on the **netted, log-scale** depth curve (positive = eroding); `U` = **item-id-keyed `read_hits`**, the registered usage proxy, ⛔ **depth never enters `U`**; *depth* = a **fitted well depth**, ⛔ **never feature importance**; ⛔ **no mass of either kind enters this record — neither inertial $M$ nor spectral $\mu$.** **Reading pointers:** Record 23 **and its C2W10 update** (the erosion story this instrument serves), Record 28 (*a gate that could not fail* — the same family of instrument defect), registry **N297 / N298 / N313 / N314** ⚠ **[dated correction 2026-08-13, `doc-curator-c2w11-claims-matrix-fold` (Part C), REPLACING IN PLACE the 2026-08-12 annotation by `doc-curator-c2w11-c2-close` — ⛔ that annotation's reasoning was WRONG and is withdrawn. What actually happened: this record's original citation read "N297 / N298 / **N302 / N303**", and those two numbers were a **FORWARD REFERENCE** to entries the C2W10 fold subsequently filed — entries that **are** on-topic for this record (the four specification/measurement defects, and the wave's prereg scorecard). The 2026-08-12 pass read the forward reference as a defect ("they did not exist when filed") and struck it; the *"different subject"* half was a real symptom of a real problem — the C2W11 fold had independently allocated the SAME numbers to its own entries (fold symbol ✰). ⚖ **The collision is resolved by the Hub's 2026-08-13 renumbering** (`N302 ✯ → N313`, `N303 ✯ → N314`, content verbatim, the C2W11 ✰ pair untouched), **and the original citation intent is RESTORED at the new numbers.** ⭐ The whole episode is filed as a registry-hygiene negative at **N315**, with the standing fix: a fold reserves its N-range in the registry header before it writes its first entry.]**, charter **Add.14 §A39.4 / §A40.1 / §A41.4**, and `PREREG-C2W10.md` AMENDMENTS 6–8 for the registered form and its two self-filed defects.

## 11.30 Three pictures from **C2W11** and the **Campaign-2 close**: **the room you can find but never enter**, **the librarian who knows the building and asks for the wrong shelf**, and **the exam we proved unpassable with a ruler instead of an attempt** **[plain-language records — new at the C2W11 close, filed 2026-08-12]**

### Picture 1 — **the room you can find but never enter**

**The physics.** A well is a basin, and a basin has a **finite capture radius**: the distance from which a damped particle actually falls in. Two numbers govern whether a memory is *usable*, and they are different numbers — **where the well is** (its address) and **how far in you must travel to reach its contents** (its payload target). This wave measured both for the first time and found the second one **larger than the basin itself**: the read launches with the payload coordinate pinned to zero (nothing may hand the read its answer), every well's target sits at `‖v_j‖ = 1.0`, and the measured capture radius is **0.8535**. Ratio **1.172 > 1** ⇒ **the needed well is outside its own basin, by arithmetic, on every query, every channel, every seed** (full-space coverage **1.0000 uncovered**).

**Why the direction of the fix is forced, not chosen.** You cannot simply widen the basin: the measured capture radius (0.8535) already sits at the **well spacing** (0.8586), and a basin materially wider than the spacing **merges with its neighbour** — the axial curvature at the midpoint of two equal wells goes non-positive below `d/s ≈ 2.01`, which the same spoke hit directly (`d/s = 0.57` at an inherited width merged every well). ⇒ **capture is capped by spacing, so only the payload target can move.**

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐ **A compositional family whose payload targets sit outside the basins that must capture them is unreadable by construction — and for reasons that have nothing to do with organization, learning, or physics.** The derived law, which outlives the wave: **`‖v_j‖ < capture_radius ≲ min-well-spacing`.** ⚠ The uncomfortable half of the record: **C2W5 half-found this and stopped** — it cut `‖v‖` from `√m ≈ 2.83` to 1.0 *because* targets were *"outside basin reach"*, and **never measured whether 1.0 was inside.** It was not. ⭐ **The generalisable habit: when you tune a quantity because it was out of range, measure whether the new value is in range.**

⭐ **And closing it moved something four waves old.** With the ratio below 1, the settle's occupancy dividend went **−0.1567 ± 0.0052 → −0.0015 ± 0.0026** (a tie; distinct wells 3.807 → 3.990). ⇒ **the *"the settle destroys launch information"* family (four waves, three rigs) is, on this substrate, a REACH ARTIFACT** — particles were being pulled toward wells they could never enter, so they scattered.

### Picture 2 — **the librarian who knows the building and asks for the wrong shelf**

**The physics.** Retrieval in a landscape has two independent parts: **the dynamics** (does a particle released near a well fall into *that* well?) and **the assertion** (does the query name the *right* well to be released near?). The program had never separated them. This wave did, with one control: launch the same particles at the **needed** wells' anchors, keep the same store, same settle, same budgets, and read with a **zero-parameter** raw sum.

| | measured |
|---|---|
| exact-set on **unseen** queries, oracle-addressed | **0.8621 ± 0.0036** (0.9961 at 4× tolerance) |
| occupancy precision | **0.9673** |
| per-particle payload error | median **0.0002** against `‖v_j‖ = 0.60` |
| the same store, **self**-addressed | **≈ 0.0004** — the floor |

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐ **The store, the settle and the payload composition all work. It composes four payloads to 0.86 the moment the addressing is correct — so the memory is not inert, not collapsed into a table, and not short of capacity.** ⛔ **Every *"the store is inert"* sentence for this substrate is now a NEVER-QUOTE.** The addressing *physics* is separately near-perfect: a particle from an asserting channel lands in the right well, inside its measured capture radius, **94.0 %** of the time. ⇒ **it is the ASSERTION that is wrong — the launch head names the wrong shelves.** ⭐ **This is the first time in five waves the program's blocker moved:** write-side organization → capture (repaired) → **the launch head's assertion, and a read that discards the continuous launch coordinate.**

⭐ **A second inference died here too.** The program had a standing rule — *a flat accuracy-vs-compute curve means the memory carries nothing*. Same store, same physics, same budgets: shipped read **flat at 0.0004**; oracle-addressed **0.0223 → 0.8219 → 0.8711**, then plateau. ⇒ ⛔ **flatness licenses only a disjunction — *"carries nothing OR cannot be addressed"* — and the two are separable by exactly this control.** (It also located the dial: everything resolvable resolves between 50 and 200 integration steps; past that the curve is flat *because it is finished*.)

### Picture 3 — **the exam we proved unpassable with a ruler instead of an attempt**

**The physics.** The wave's question was whether physics-trained organization beats a matched non-physics organizer. An **organizer moves wells; it cannot move the encoder.** So *any* placement organizer is really choosing an **assignment of wells to cue sites** — and the best possible such assignment is not a matter of opinion: it is the optimum of a linear assignment problem on the label-free co-occurrence matrix, and it can be **solved exactly, in minutes, with no store at all.**

| bound | value |
|---|---|
| best **bijective** re-placement of wells onto cue sites — **the ceiling on ANY placement organizer** | **0.3290 ± 0.0074** |
| the launch points' own **decodability**, recomputed independently by a second, blind spoke | **0.3449 ± 0.0175** |
| expected correct wells **at the ceiling** | **1.32 of 4** — and exact-set needs ~4 of 4 |

**→ CLU, said plainly.** ⭐⭐ **Both arms are floored by the same cap, so the swap compares nothing: the question is UNASKABLE at this address dimension — not lost, unaskable.** ⛔ *"The organizer swap failed"* and *"physics lost the swap"* are **NEVER-QUOTE in every form.** The mechanism is measured rather than argued: **~2/3 of unseen combinations deflate to a pick-tuple another combination also produces**, and the collision is **structural, not noise** (noiseless launches 0.3424 vs as-launched 0.3372 — the query jitter costs only 0.005), so **a quieter query cannot buy it back.** ⭐ **The two ceilings were produced by two spokes running concurrently and blind to each other, on two different instruments, and they agree inside their error bars** — which is why the bound is quotable at all.

⭐⭐ **The transferable lesson, and it is an epistemic one.** *When an experiment keeps returning zero, ask whether the question has an upper bound you can compute without running it.* A `scipy` call on a matrix answered in minutes what five waves of building could not — and turned a fifth consecutive null into **a bound with a named next dial**.

**Status: [⭐⭐ MECHANICS wave, Head-ratified CLOSED; ⛔ the tier-ii VALUE surface — generalization, calibration, navigability — is a DECLARED NOT-RUN, never a null and never a loss] · [⛔ TOY substrate, now formally DEMOTED to a smoke/regression instrument and NEVER a claim venue] · [⛔ no paper number, no benchmark result, no full-CLU verdict; the score remains 0 external benchmarks won] · [⛔ the oracle-addressed 0.8621 is a DIAGNOSTIC — it hands the read its addressing and is never a claim] · [⚠ the settle's information-neutrality carries three qualifiers: neutral not positive · measured on an UNORGANIZED store · a CANDIDATE mechanism for the open §A31.2 question, NOT a discharge] · [⚠ K4-at-full-ψ passes but is SOUND-BUT-WEAK — every leg reads 0.0000 because the read carries nothing to leak through; it must be re-run wherever the read leaves the floor] · [⛔ depth is not feature importance (§A23.5 ACTIVE and TRAVELLING)].**
**Nomenclature (Def-2):** *capture radius* = the measured **SC-6** radius from which a damped particle settles to the site — ⛔ never inferred from `λ_min`, which is positive at wells with capture radius exactly zero; *spacing* = median nearest-neighbour distance over the **store** population (⛔ never a sizing set — that mismatch produced a retracted mechanism); *`d/s`* = well separation over **measured** width, with `α‖q‖²` subtracted (a 1.44× inflation otherwise); ⛔ **no mass of either kind enters this record — neither inertial $M$ nor spectral $\mu$** (the kinetics selectors remain ratified designs, unbuilt).
**Reading pointers:** Record 11.21 (*the settle that merges* — the picture this wave re-priced), Record 11.27 (*the memory that answers and still loses*), Record 11.28 (*the test made out of the thing it was testing* — the same instrument family as this wave's circular gate), registry **N302–N311**, charter **Add.13 §A35–§A38**, **Add.15 §A42–§A43**, and **Add.16 §A44–§A45** for the Campaign-3 pivot.
